# Ainize UX Test Scenarios (235)

This document lists 235 user-experience test scenarios for **Ainize** (ai-nize = AI + -ize): a P2P marketplace where verified knowledge is plugged into an AI model. Every scenario is grounded in the current code (web routes, i18n dictionaries, node API, CLI, agent) and executable on the live demo. A machine-readable copy lives next to this file: `docs/ux-test-scenarios.json` (this file is generated from it by `scripts/render-ux-scenarios.py`).

## How to use

- **Live demo:** three nodes on one machine — `http://localhost:3402` (node-a: web UI + seller + verifier + serving), `http://localhost:3403` (node-b: verifier), `http://localhost:3404` (node-c: verifier + serving) — sharing a local AIN dev chain (`http://localhost:8081`, app `/apps/knowledge`) and one vLLM model server (`http://localhost:8000`, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next). Every node also serves the web UI.
- **Demo knowledge ids:** `krx-all-2761` (LISTED / "Verified"), `krx-all-2761-ep12`, `krx-all-2761-ep6`, `pixelplus-087600` (SUPERSEDED — the chip reads "Newer version: krx-all-2761" on Explore and the knowledge page, "Newer version available" where no successor id is passed; still purchasable and testable). Names shown in the UI are English (`KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)`, `Pixelplus ticker code (single fact)`, …). Right after a fresh cluster start the items are still ANNOUNCED/VERIFYING for a few minutes; most scenarios assume verification has finished (`GET /api/info` → `counts.listed = 1`).
- **Addresses are live values.** Node identities are regenerated by `scripts/cluster-restart.sh --fresh`. Read the node-a address from `GET http://localhost:3402/api/info` (`node.address`; at the time of writing `0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5`), node-b/node-c from `/api/nodes`. Detail URLs have the form `/<creator address>/<knowledge id>`; the page resolves by id, so an outdated address segment still renders.
- **Operator password flow:** the first visit to `/signing` on a node shows "Create the operator password" (`needsSetup`); afterwards it shows "Sign in to your node". The same password works for the CLI (`ainize login --password …`; the first CLI login also sets it). Visitors never need to sign in — the sign-in page says so, and `/new-patch` shows a public pre-screen when signed out.
- **Teach mode (AZ-101…AZ-122, TM-090/091):** run against a node with `teach.enabled: true` and `backend: 'stub'` (+ `stubOffline: true` when no model server is available) so no GPU job is ever started — e.g. the dev node `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home ~/.ngram-teach/node-t start` on `http://localhost:3412` (operator password `teach-pass`). Browser specs: `packages/e2e/tests/web-teach.spec.ts` (visitor flow) and `web-teach-operator.spec.ts` (landing / sign-in / pre-screen / Teaching tab): `AINIZE_URL=http://localhost:3412 AINIZE_PASS=teach-pass npx playwright test tests/web-teach*.spec.ts --project=web`.
- **CLI:** run `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home ~/.ngram-cluster/node-a …` from `/mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace` (or the globally installed `ainize`). The agent is `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js`. Node 24: `export PATH="$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH"`.
- **Currency and quota:** prices are shown in AIN (local dev chain; fund test accounts with `ainize chain fund <address> <amount>`). Anonymous live tests are limited to 20 per IP per hour; operators are unlimited.
- **Language:** English is the default UI language; the header button `한국어` switches to Korean (persisted in `localStorage.ainize.locale`). All quoted UI strings are the English (`en`) values of `packages/web/src/i18n/**`.
- **Format:** each scenario has an id (`AZ-001`…`AZ-122`, plus `TM-090`/`TM-091` filed by the multi-knowledge work), persona, imperative title, goal, priority (P0 must-pass / P1 important / P2 nice-to-have), area, automation hint (manual / api / cli / e2e), preconditions, numbered steps, observable expected results and evidence (source files / dictionary keys). Scenarios are ordered by persona, then priority.

## Visitor (knowledge user)

### AZ-001 - Read the landing hero and follow the two primary calls to action

**Goal:** A first-time visitor understands what Ainize is and can jump to Explore or Live test without signing in.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** landing - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session (open a private window)
- Node http://localhost:3402 is running with the demo catalog fully verified (1 LISTED, 3 SUPERSEDED — GET /api/info counts.listed = 1, counts.verifying = 0)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/
2. Read the top navigation and the hero block
3. Hover the purple count card title to read its tooltip
4. Click the primary pill 'Explore knowledge'
5. Go back and click the secondary pill 'Try a live test'

**Expected**

- Nav shows 'Explore knowledge', 'Live test', 'Node sign-in' (tooltip: 'Console for node operators and developers. You do not need to sign in to use knowledge.') and a locale button reading '한국어'
- Hero title 'Plug knowledge into your AI' and sub 'Ainize = AI + -ize, "make it usable by AI". Pick verified knowledge, check it live, load it into your model in seconds.'
- Count card reads '1 verified knowledge' (info.counts.listed); no 'being verified' sub-line is rendered because counts.verifying is 0 (the sub-line only appears while items are ANNOUNCED/VERIFYING); the card tooltip reads 'Knowledge that independent verifier nodes loaded into the real model and checked for accuracy and side effects. (verifier quorum reached (≥ 2 independent attestations))'
- Card note: 'No sign-up · pays automatically with a wallet (AIN) or node credit · removable any time'
- 'Explore knowledge' navigates to /explore; 'Try a live test' navigates to /chat (which redirects to /chat/krx-all-2761, the first testable item)

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/LandingPage.tsx (IntroSection, CountCard, PillRow)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/public.ts: landing.hero.title, landing.hero.sub, landing.hero.count, landing.hero.count_verifying, landing.hero.note, landing.nav.signin_help`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/common.ts: common.locale`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/info counts {patches, listed, verifying, superseded, rejected}`

### AZ-002 - Inspect the trending card for the only verified knowledge

**Goal:** The 'Popular verified knowledge' section shows only LISTED items with human units, accuracy and an AIN price note.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** landing - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session
- krx-all-2761 is LISTED with two executed attestations (26/26); run only after both verifiers attested (GET /api/patches/krx-all-2761 passed = 2)
- node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5; it changes on a `cluster-restart.sh --fresh`)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/ and scroll to 'Popular verified knowledge'
2. Count the cards and read the single card's lines
3. Hover 'facts covered', 'accuracy' and 'Verified' labels for tooltips
4. Click the card
5. Go back and click 'See all'

**Expected**

- Sub-title 'Passed independent verification and ready to load into the model.'
- Exactly 1 card (catalog status=LISTED): name 'KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)', meta 'Creator: node-a · Qwen3.8-Flash-Next'
- Lines: 'facts covered' → '2,761 facts'; 'accuracy' → '100% on a 26-question sample of 2,761' (the percentage is printed against the denominator the verifiers actually used, next to the coverage the anchor claims) with a green score bar; 'Verified' → 'Verified (2/2 independent verifiers)'; 'Price' → '25 AIN' with note 'AIN = AI Network token (this demo runs a local dev chain)'
- SUPERSEDED items (krx-all-2761-ep12, -ep6, pixelplus-087600) do not appear
- Clicking the card opens /0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5/krx-all-2761 (the node-a address segment); 'See all' opens /explore

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/LandingPage.tsx useCatalogQuery({status:'LISTED', sort:'popular', limit:6}), TrendCard`
- `packages/web/src/components/public/PatchListItem.tsx executedAccuracy, usePriceLabel, useVerificationLabel`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/public.ts: landing.trending.*, item.verified_by, item.price_ain`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/common.ts: units.facts, price.ain_note`
- `packages/node/src/seed.ts (English knowledge names)`

### AZ-003 - Browse the Explore list and read every field of a knowledge row

**Goal:** A visitor sees all non-draft knowledge with status, units, verification count, accuracy and price without technical jargon.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** explore - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session
- Demo catalog: 4 items, all topic krx-ticker-codes, model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next; krx-all-2761 LISTED, the other three SUPERSEDED
- node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/explore
2. Read the title, intro, the count line and the line under it about hidden versions
3. Read the row for 'KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)'
4. Click 'show' on that line to bring the retired versions back
5. Read the row for 'Pixelplus ticker code (single fact)'
6. Look at the pagination bar at the bottom
7. Click the first row

**Expected**

- Title 'Explore knowledge'; intro 'Choose by verification status and accuracy. You can check any of it with a live test before buying.'; Explore opens on 'Show: Current only', so the count reads '1 knowledge' and the line under it reads '3 older versions hidden · show' (tooltip '"Current only" hides knowledge that a newer version replaced and knowledge that failed verification.'); clicking 'show' switches to 'All versions' and the count becomes '4 knowledge' with the hidden line gone
- Row krx-all-2761: name followed by the purple 'Verified' certified label and a green status chip 'For sale' (tooltip 'On sale as the current version for this topic. Whether it passed verification is what the "Verified" badge beside it says.') — one word no longer means two things on the same row; ident 'node-a / krx-all-2761'; 'Creator: node-a · Target model: Qwen3.8-Flash-Next · Topic: krx-ticker-codes'; '2,761 facts · 270,053 memory entries · Size 331.7 MB · 0 downloads'; 'Verified (2/2 independent verifiers) · 100% (26/26 checked) · asked as template + chat' — the denominator is the attestation's own score and the form is the one its verifiers used
- Row pixelplus-087600 (visible once 'All versions' is on): status chip 'Newer version: krx-all-2761' in the warning palette (#8a4b00 on #fff3e0), '8 facts · 2,992 memory entries', 'Verified (2/2 independent verifiers) · 100% (4/4 checked) · asked as template + natural', price '0.1 AIN'
- The 56 px seal on each card carries information: full colour ([data-testid=seal-sealed], filter none) only on the verified current version, greyed ([data-testid=seal-retired], grayscale(1), opacity .45) on a superseded one, and absent on anything that never reached quorum — not the same purple seal on all four rows
- No UI label shows raw words like 'rows', 'patch', 'LISTED' or 'SUPERSEDED' (the creator-written description text of the krx items may mention file names such as rows-pin.npz — that is data, not a label)
- Pagination shows 'First' ‹ '1 / 1' › 'Last' with all four buttons disabled (10 items per page)
- Clicking a row opens /{creator address}/{id}, i.e. /0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5/krx-all-2761

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/components/public/PatchListItem.tsx`
- `packages/web/src/components/ui/Misc.tsx Pagination ('First'/'Last'), StatusChip, Certified`

### AZ-004 - Read the knowledge detail header and stat strip

**Goal:** The detail page explains status, verification count, price unit and size in plain words and links to Live test and the same-subject page.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** patch - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session
- krx-all-2761 LISTED, passed 2/2, revenue 0, downloads 0
- node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5; the page resolves by knowledge id, so an outdated address segment still renders)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5/krx-all-2761
2. Read the white header band (title, ID line, track line, right-hand buttons)
3. Read the status row and meta line under the band
4. Read each stat in the strip and hover 'accuracy', 'Memory entries', 'Facts'
5. Click 'Other knowledge on this subject ›'
6. Go back and click the 'Live test' button

**Expected**

- Header: title 'KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)', 'ID krx-all-2761', 'Creator: node-a', 'Track finance/KRX-latest · topic finance/krx'
- Right side: purple button 'Live test' with sub-text 'Compare answers before and after'; link 'Other knowledge on this subject ›'
- Status chip 'For sale' followed by 'Verified on the real model 2/2 · Verified' — the chip is the listing state, the sentence is the verification
- Meta: 'By node-a · target model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next · verified {n}m ago' (or '{n}h ago' depending on age)
- Stats: 'Purchases' 0; 'accuracy' (lowercase glossary term) reads the measured pair '1/8 → 26/26' (tooltip 'The same verification run scored 1/8 before the knowledge was loaded and 26/26 after.') with the note '100% after loading' — the percentage alone could not say whether the model already knew the answers; 'Memory entries' 270,053; 'Facts' 2,761; 'Size' 331.7 MB; 'Price' '25 AIN' with note 'AIN = AI Network token (this demo runs a local dev chain)'; 'Revenue' renders 'Free' for the zero amount (shared price formatter) — flag as a copy issue if judged confusing
- Tab bar: 'Overview', 'Verification', 'Origins & derivatives', 'Buy', 'History'
- Subject link opens /benchmarks/krx-ticker-codes; 'Live test' opens /chat/krx-all-2761

### AZ-005 - Read the Verification tab and confirm only real-model runs count

**Goal:** A visitor can see who verified the knowledge, how, which results counted, and what actually backs a verification.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** verification - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session
- krx-all-2761 has two executed attestations (node-b and node-c; verified_on vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next; restarts 0); verifier addresses come from GET /api/nodes (currently node-b 0x430b3A37…9935, node-c 0x199Ff870…bce9)

**Steps**

1. Open the detail page of krx-all-2761 and click the 'Verification' tab
2. Read the summary row
3. Read both table rows; hover the 'Side-effect check' and 'Counts' cells
4. Read the three explanatory notes under the table

**Expected**

- Summary: 'Run on the real model' 2/2; 'Integrity only' 0; 'Status' chip 'For sale'
- Table headers: 'Verifier node', 'Method', 'Before', 'Accuracy', 'Side-effect check', 'Restarts detected', 'Counts', 'Result', 'Time' — 'Before' is score.pre_apply, which every attestation carries and which used to reach the DOM only inside a title= attribute; 'Counts' replaced the old 'Deposit' column, which reported a bond that was never escrowed (critique 2 item 127)
- Rows for node-b and node-c (name with the shortened address underneath): Method 'run on the real model'; Before '1/8' (the same run's pre_apply, tooltip 'What the same verifier scored on the same questions BEFORE the knowledge was loaded (pre_apply) …'); Accuracy '26/26'; Side-effect check 'not reported' (no collateral_nat in the record); Restarts detected 'none'; Counts 'independent' (tooltip: the result is signed with the verifier node's key, no deposit is escrowed, any node can challenge it); Result 'Passed' in green; Time as '{n}h ago' with the full date in the tooltip
- Note 1 starts 'Verified — Only verifications run on the real model count toward Verified (currently 2/2). The 0 integrity-only checks are shown separately…'
- A note under the table explains the pair: '"Before" and "Accuracy" are the same questions scored twice in the same run — before the knowledge was loaded (pre_apply) and after it. The pair is what shows how much the knowledge changed; the second number alone cannot.'
- Note 2 starts 'Restarts detected: if the model server restarted mid-run…'; Note 3 is the honest backing statement: 'No deposit is at stake on a verification. Each result is signed with the verifier node's key and stays on the public record under that identity for ever. Any node that thinks a result is wrong can challenge it, and a challenge stops the sale until the knowledge is re-verified.'

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/PatchPage.tsx Verification(), sideEffectCell()`
- `packages/web/src/pages/detail/recordText.ts howLabel()`
- `packages/node/src/verifier.ts (only executed attestations count; hash-only separate)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/detail.ts: detail.ver.*, detail.how.executed, detail.pass`
- `GET /api/patches/krx-all-2761 attestations`
- `packages/core/src/catalog.ts deriveCatalog (self-attestations excluded; the fraction is clamped to the quorum)`

### AZ-006 - Read the Buy tab as a visitor and probe the automatic-payment address

**Goal:** A visitor understands how automatic payment works, sees the price quote address, and is told sign-in is needed to buy from this node.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session
- krx-all-2761 LISTED (quorum 2/2) with gateway_url http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761 — while it is still VERIFYING the gateway answers HTTP 423 'patch not listed yet (verification 1/2)' instead of 402

**Steps**

1. Open the detail page of krx-all-2761 and click the 'Buy' tab
2. Read the 'Buy with automatic payment' section and its key/value rows
3. Click the 'Purchase address' link (opens a new tab) and inspect the response
4. Expand 'For node operators & developers: buy from the command line / API'
5. Read the 'Buy from this node' section
6. Click 'Operator & developer docs ›'

**Expected**

- Heading 'Buy with automatic payment' with the explanation starting 'Ask for the knowledge and the selling node quotes a price; once payment is confirmed you download immediately. Pay with a wallet (AIN) or node credit — no sign-up…'
- Rows: 'Price' '25 AIN · pay once per download' with note 'AIN = AI Network token (this demo runs a local dev chain)'; 'Selling node' 'node-a 0xF7A9dE49…A1F5' (name + shortened node-a address); 'Purchase address' http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761
- The new tab returns HTTP 402 with an x-payment-required header (price quote), not the file
- Details show comment lines '# 1) ask → price quote comes back', '# 2) pay, then retry with the proof', '# or let the CLI do the whole loop' (ainize patch buy krx-all-2761), '# AI agent: detect unknown answer → search → auto-pay → load'
- 'Buy from this node' shows only 'Sign in as this node’s operator to buy with the node’s wallet.' — no Buy button, no 'Paying…' state for a visitor
- Blue box 'Buy with an AI agent — An AI agent can buy on your behalf…' and the docs link opens /docs

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/PatchPage.tsx Buy() (isOperator=false branch)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/detail.ts: detail.buy.*`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts x402 402 flow; packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id (423 while not listed)`
- `GET /api/patches/krx-all-2761 gateway_url`

### AZ-007 - Open Live test, pick knowledge and use the sample-question chips

**Goal:** A visitor can start a live test without login, see what is testable, and pre-fill a benchmark question with one click.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session
- Model server on node-a is available (GET /api/chat/patches runtime.available=true)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/chat
2. Read the title, model chip, subtitle and the left panel
3. Tick 'Pixelplus ticker code (single fact)' in the left list (checkbox), untick it, then tick 'KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)'
4. In the composer read 'Sample questions this knowledge answers' and hover the first chip
5. Click 'Show 18 more', then 'Show less'
6. Click the chip '종목코드 삼성전자' (do not send)
7. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/does-not-exist

**Expected**

- URL is replaced with /chat/krx-all-2761 (first testable item); title 'Live test'; chip 'Test model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next'; subtitle 'Ask the same question before and after loading the knowledge and watch the answer change. It loads and unloads in seconds, no restart.'
- Left panel 'Knowledge to load (pick up to 3)' (checkboxes, help 'They load in the order you tick them. If two overlap, the one ticked last wins.') with hint 'Only knowledge whose body is on this node can be tested.'; 4 items each showing name, 'node-a/{id}', '{n} facts', '100% accuracy', price like '25 AIN' + 'AIN = AI Network token (this demo runs a local dev chain)', a status chip ('For sale' for the current version, 'Newer version available' for a retired one); the active item shows 'Selected'
- Ticking exactly one item changes the URL to /chat/{id} (several → /chat/{id1},{id2}) and the main header shows its name, status chip, '{n} facts' and a 'Details →' link; the transcript shows 'No questions yet' with 'Click a sample question below or type your own. You get two answers side by side: before and after loading the knowledge.'
- krx-all-2761 shows 8 chips + 'Show 18 more' (26 samples); the chip for '종목코드 픽셀플러스' carries 'Expected: 087600' both as its tooltip and as a visible second line inside the chip (its accessible name stays the bare prompt); 'Show less' collapses back
- Clicking a chip puts its text into the textarea (placeholder 'Type a question and press Enter (Shift+Enter for a new line)') and focuses it; footer reads 'Free tries are limited per hour. No sign-in needed.'
- Unknown id: yellow alert 'The knowledge in the address (does-not-exist) cannot be tested on this node, so the first one in the list was chosen.' and URL becomes /chat/krx-all-2761

### AZ-008 - Run a Compare test and read the correct-answer marker and quota counter

**Goal:** A visitor sees the before/after answers side by side, an automatic ✓/✗ against the sealed expected answer, and how many free tries remain.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session; fresh IP quota (fewer than 20 tests this hour)
- Model server available; no other test running

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761
2. Confirm 'View' is on 'Compare' and 'Enable thinking' is unchecked
3. Click the chip '종목코드 삼성전자' and press Enter
4. While pending, read the two bubbles and the row above the composer
5. Wait for both answers
6. Read the badges in each bubble header and hover the badge in 'After loading'
7. Read the composer footer

**Expected**

- Pending state: two bubbles 'Before loading' and 'After loading' with animated dots and note 'Includes loading and unloading — this can take tens of seconds.'; the row 'Waiting for the answer — you can cancel if it takes too long.' with a 'Cancel' button; the Send button reads 'Waiting for the answer…'; chips, mode buttons and textarea are disabled
- HTTP 200 from POST /api/chat with mode 'compare'; 'After loading' shows 'reply {n}s' and '· loaded in {n}s' (or '· was already loaded')
- 'After loading' badge '✓ Correct' (answer contains 005930) with tooltip 'This question is one of the knowledge’s benchmark items, so the answer was checked automatically. Expected: 005930'; 'Before loading' shows '✓ Correct' or '✗ Wrong' depending on the base model; BOTH bubbles render 'Expected: 005930' as muted text under the verdict, so the tick or cross can be checked without a mouse (data-testid chat-expected-base / chat-expected-patched)

### AZ-009 - Exhaust the 20-per-hour free trial and read the quota message

**Goal:** A visitor is told clearly when free tries are used up and how to continue (wait an hour or buy).

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- No operator session; quota for this IP is fresh (no anonymous live test from this host in the last hour)
- Model server available; pixelplus-087600 is testable (body on node-a, status LISTED or SUPERSEDED — not still ANNOUNCED)
- Browser and curl run on the same host and use the same hostname (http://localhost) so req.ip matches (IPv4 vs IPv6 loopback would count as different visitors)
- Use 'Before only' view to keep each request fast

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/pixelplus-087600 and select 'Before only'
2. Send the chip '종목코드 픽셀플러스' 20 times in a row (wait for each answer), watching the footer counter
3. After the 20th answer read the page
4. Try to type in the textarea and try one more send via curl: POST http://localhost:3402/api/chat with {"patch_id":"pixelplus-087600","mode":"base","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"hi"}]}

**Expected**

- Footer counts down 'Free trial 19/20 left this hour' … 'Free trial 1/20 left this hour'
- After the 20th answer (remaining_quota 0) a yellow alert appears above the grid: 'You used all free tries for this hour. Try again in an hour or buy the knowledge.' and the textarea is disabled with that same text as placeholder; chips and Send are disabled
- A further POST /api/chat from the same IP returns HTTP 429 with message 'quota_chat: free live-test quota exhausted for this hour — buy the patch or run your own node'; the 429 body also carries quota_reset (epoch ms = the window start plus the hour); in the UI that maps to the red turn error 'You used all free tries for this hour. Try again in an hour, or buy the knowledge and use it without limits on your own node.' with NO Retry button (send() would return at the exhausted guard without issuing a request) — in its place a link 'Buy this knowledge' to the knowledge page and the measured reset time 'Free tries reset at HH:MM'
- The quota is 20 requests per visitor IP in a fixed 60-minute window that starts at the visitor's first request (it resets once 3,600,000 ms have passed since the window began); an operator session is unlimited ('You are signed in as this node’s operator — unlimited tests.')

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/chat (chatQuota(visitor, 20, 3600_000), HttpError 429)`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts chatQuota()`
- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx exhausted state, composerDisabled, mapChatError 429 → chat.err.quota`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts: chat.quota.left_of, chat.quota.none, chat.err.quota, chat.quota.operator`

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** ledger - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session
- Node-a ledger is the AIN dev chain (kind 'ain', app /apps/knowledge); all four demo items verified so the chain holds ~20 records (4 anchor, 8 attest, 3 supersede, 3 node, 2 branch) — read the live count from GET /api/info ledger.records

**Steps**

6. Choose 'All records', scroll to 'Origin → derivative map', hover boxes and click the box 'krx-all-2761'

**Expected**

- Title 'Public record' with description 'A record anyone can check: who registered, verified and bought which knowledge.'; header badge next to the logo reads 'AI Network'
- Info card: 'Record type' 'AIN blockchain'; 'Network' ain:local; 'Records' {n} (= ledger.records); 'Blocks recorded' (chain height, increasing); 'Connected AI Network node' http://localhost:8081; 'Integrity' 'valid · {n} checked' in green
- Developer row: 'Stored at' 'NODE OPERATORS & DEVELOPERS /apps/knowledge — Recorded on the AI Network — registrations as knowledge entries; …'; 'Open in explorer' opens http://localhost:8081/get_value?ref=%2Fapps%2Fknowledge (URL-encoded href; visible text unencoded) in a new tab (JSON)

GET /api/ledger returns the NEWEST `limit` records and cannot page further back, so when the ledger holds more than the page asked for (1,000) a line above the table reads 'Showing the most recent 1,000 of {n} records — the node returns this many at a time and cannot page further back.'; with a smaller ledger that line is absent
- Select options: 'All records', 'Registered', 'Verification', 'Purchase settled', 'Knowledge track', 'Node', 'Newer version', 'Subscription', 'Re-verification request'; 'Purchase settled' → 'No "Purchase settled" records yet.' (until a purchase has been made)
- 'Newer version' → one row per supersede record on the WHOLE ledger (the node applies the filter, not the fetched window): e.g. 'krx-all-2761 marked as the newer version of krx-all-2761-ep6 (241,992 overlapping memory entries)'
- Map: 4 boxes in three columns — left krx-all-2761-ep6 and pixelplus-087600, middle krx-all-2761-ep12, right krx-all-2761 (label 'For sale · Qwen3.8-Flash-N…' — the map prints the same listing state as the chips); two grey solid 'extends' arrows (krx-all-2761 → ep12, ep12 → ep6) and three orange dashed 'supersedes' arrows; legend 'derived from origin (creator revenue share)', 'replaced by a newer version (same subject, overlapping entries)', 'origins on the left, derivatives to the right'; clicking a box opens that knowledge's detail page

2. Hover each 'View' option to read its help, then click 'After only'

4. Click 'Before only', click the chip '종목코드 HMM' (under 'Show 18 more') and press Enter; wait
5. Tick 'Enable thinking' and read the help text next to it; untick it
6. Click 'Clear conversation'

**Expected**

- Help tooltips: Compare → 'See answers before and after side by side. Includes loading and unloading, so it takes a bit longer.'; After only → 'Only the answer with the knowledge loaded.'; Before only → 'Only the original model’s answer.'
- 'After only' turn renders a single full-width bubble 'After loading'; since the prompt is not a benchmark sample the header shows 'Free question — not auto-scored' instead of ✓/✗
- 'Before only' turn renders a single bubble 'Before loading' with 'reply {n}ms/s' and a ✓/✗ badge (sample matched, expected 011200); no 'loaded in' meta
- With thinking on the helper text switches to 'Thinking is on — replies are slower'; off shows 'Off by default. When on, the model reasons at length before answering, so replies are slower. Keep it off for short factual questions.'
- 'Clear conversation' empties the transcript back to 'No questions yet' for this knowledge only; the quota counter keeps its value

**Evidence**

### AZ-019 - Stop a slow live test — and be told whether it cost a free try

**Goal:** A visitor is never stuck waiting: an in-flight test can be cancelled and re-sent from the transcript.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session; quota not exhausted
- Model server available

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761 with 'Compare' selected
2. Click 'Show 18 more', click the chip '종목코드 유라클' and press Enter
3. Within the first seconds click 'Cancel' (or 'Stop waiting' while the turn is queued) in the row under the transcript
4. Read the turn in the transcript
5. Click 'Retry' on that turn and wait for completion

**Expected**

- After 'Cancel' the pending bubbles are replaced by a red alert and a 'Retry' button; the composer is enabled again immediately
- The alert says which of the two happened: cancelled while still QUEUED → 'You stopped waiting. The node had not started this test yet, so no free try was used.' (POST /api/chat/cancel answers {cancelled:true, reason:'queued', charged:false} and the node returns 499 without touching the model); cancelled while RUNNING → 'You stopped waiting, but the test had already started on the shared model, so it still counts as one free try.'
- No duplicate turn is created; the cancelled turn is not sent back as history to the model
- 'Retry' re-sends the same prompt with the same view/thinking settings, replaces the errored turn in place, and finishes with '✓ Correct' (expected 088340) in 'After loading' — a request cancelled while it was already running is still processed and charged, so Retry queues behind it
- Leaving the page (e.g. clicking 'Explore knowledge' in the header) during a pending request aborts it without console errors

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx cancel(), retry(), inflight abort on unmount, mapChatError AbortError → chat.err.cancelled`
- `packages/web/src/components/chat/TurnView.tsx error row with 'Retry'`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts: chat.input.cancel, chat.err.cancelled, chat.turn.retry`
- `packages/node/src/chat-queue.ts cancel() (queued = free, running = charged); packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/chat/cancel`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts: chat.queue.stop_waiting, chat.queue.cancelled, chat.queue.cancelled_late`

### AZ-020 - See who holds the shared model, and that your own test is queued behind it

**Goal:** A visitor whose test is waiting behind the shared model can see that it is queued (not failed), who holds the model and for how long, and can stop waiting.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session; quota not exhausted (both tabs on one browser/IP share the 20/hour quota)
- Model server available
- Two browser tabs (or two devices) on http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761

**Steps**

1. In tab A select 'Compare', tick 'Enable thinking', click 'Show 18 more', click the chip '종목코드 한독' and press Enter
2. Within ~20 s switch to tab B and reload it (or wait for its 20-second poll)
3. Read the left panel of tab B
4. In tab B send '종목코드 HMM' with 'Before only' while tab A is still pending
5. Wait for both tabs to finish; reload tab B after tab A completes

**Expected**

- While tab A's OWN request holds the lock, tab A shows 'Your test has the shared model — started {n}s ago' (D3: it used to show the 'another test is running, try again in a moment' box about itself)
- Tab B shows a blue status box: 'Someone else is testing on the shared model right now.' with 'Another test in progress (chat:krx-all-2761, node process {pid}) — started {n}s ago' and 'The model loads and unloads one knowledge at a time, so tests run one after another.'; the elapsed time ticks every second instead of freezing until the next 20 s poll
- GET /api/chat/patches returns a non-null lock {owner:'pid:<node pid>', label:'chat:krx-all-2761', since, alive:true, stale:false, mine:true} plus `now` (the node clock) and `queue` while tab A's request holds the runtime lock
- Tab B's own pending turn says so in the transcript: 'Queued behind another test — your question has not been lost.', the holder line, a ticking 'waiting {n}s', and a 'Stop waiting' button (GET /api/chat/status?request_id= reports state 'queued' with position 1 within ~1.5 s)
- Tab B's request is serialized (queued in-process behind A): it completes after tab A's answer with the correct result; the 'The shared model stayed busy for too long, so this request gave up waiting.' error only appears if the lock wait exceeds 20 minutes (HTTP 503 + Retry-After, no longer a 500)
- Once tab A finishes, the lock banner disappears from tab B on the next poll (≤ 20 s) and lock is null again
- A lock file left behind by a node that was killed is NOT shown as a live test: lock.alive is false and the picker shows 'A previous test left the shared model marked as busy; the next test clears it automatically.'

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/runtime.ts lockHolder() (alive/stale/mine), queueState(), serial() onEnter, acquireLock()`
- `packages/node/src/chat-queue.ts ChatQueue (queued → running → gone, per-visitor tickets)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/chat/patches (lock, now, queue), GET /api/chat/status, POST /api/chat/cancel`
- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx useChatStatusQuery(1.5 s poll), queue view, cancel()`
- `packages/web/src/components/chat/TurnView.tsx QueuePending; KnowledgePicker.tsx lock banner variants; util.ts lockKind/useSince/useTicker`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts: chat.lock.busy, chat.lock.holder, chat.lock.mine, chat.lock.stale, chat.lock.help, chat.queue.*, chat.err.busy`

### AZ-021 - Handle the model-server-off state on Live test and Network

**Goal:** When the node's model server is down a visitor gets a clear reason and no dead controls.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** manual

**Preconditions**

- No operator session
- Test coordinator has stopped or paused the vLLM model server behind http://localhost:8000 (e.g. `docker pause flashnext`); GET /api/chat/patches → runtime.available=false after the 30 s status cache expires; restore it afterwards (`docker unpause flashnext`)

**Steps**

1. Wait ~60 s after stopping the server (30 s runtime status cache + 20 s page poll), then open http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761
2. Read the left panel and try to click a knowledge item
3. Read the textarea placeholder and try to click a sample chip
4. Open http://localhost:3402/network and read the 'Model server' card
5. Restart the model server, wait ~60 s and reload /chat (or wait for the 20-second poll)

**Expected**

- Left panel shows a yellow status box 'The model server is off right now, so testing is unavailable.' with 'It comes back once the node operator starts the model server.'; every knowledge item is disabled (greyed, tooltip with the same text)
- Textarea is disabled with placeholder 'The model server is off right now, so testing is unavailable.'; chips and Send are disabled; no request is sent
- Network → 'Model server' card: 'Status' shows the runtime error text 'serving API unreachable' with a grey dot (rt.error takes precedence over 'unavailable'), 'Model' '—', 'Live connection' 'not connected'
- After the server is back (up to ~50 s: 30 s cache + 20 s / 15 s polls), the picker re-enables automatically and the Network card shows 'available — knowledge can be loaded live' and 'connected — load and unload without restart'
- If a request was already in flight when the server dropped, the turn shows 'The model server is off or not responding. Try again in a moment.' with 'Retry'

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx runtimeOff, composerDisabled/disabledReason, pollingInterval 20_000`
- `packages/web/src/components/chat/KnowledgePicker.tsx runtimeOff alert and disabled items`
- `packages/web/src/pages/NetworkPage.tsx runtime card (rt.error ?? runtime_down)`
- `packages/node/src/runtime.ts status() (30 s statusCache, error 'serving API unreachable')`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts: chat.runtime.off, chat.runtime.off_detail, chat.err.runtime`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/detail.ts: detail.net.runtime_down, detail.net.hook_no, detail.net.runtime_ok, detail.net.hook_ok`

### AZ-223 - A runaway answer is cut off with a plain explanation, not shown as an endless loop

**Goal:** When the model gets stuck repeating itself, the visitor sees the useful part of the answer, one sentence explaining what happened, and can still open the raw text.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Model server available; krx-all-2761 testable on the node
- No operator session needed

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761, choose 'After only'
2. Send the one-character prompt '드' — with the shipped stop sequences the chat path answers normally; confirm the answer does NOT run away and is NOT auto-scored
3. As the operator, POST /api/runtime/complete {"prompt":"드","max_tokens":200} — the completion path still degenerates and is where the guard is observable end to end
4. Repeat that call with {"raw": true} to see the same request without the stop sequences and without the guard
5. Back in the UI, on a bubble that carries the note, click 'Show the raw answer (all {n} characters)'

**Expected**

- The answer is cut at the point the repetition starts; underneath it a yellow note reads 'The model started repeating itself, so the answer is cut off here — that usually means the question is outside what this knowledge covers.' with 'showing {shown} of {raw} characters'
- 'Show the raw answer' reveals the model's full output unchanged — nothing is ever deleted, only truncated
- POST /api/chat returns patched.truncated = 'repetition' with shown_chars < raw_chars and raw_content carrying the full text
- On /api/runtime/complete the guarded call returns truncated='repetition' while the same call with raw:true returns the whole loop, unflagged
- The chat path is the quiet one: over PROBE A's 73 prompts re-run through this node, 0 of 73 chat answers ran away while the completion path caught and cut 8. '드' answering normally in the UI is the expected result of this scenario, not a failure of it
- A short correct answer (e.g. '087600') is never flagged: truncated is null and no note is shown
- Nor is any other complete short answer: a big round number ('1000000000000000'), a 60-dash rule, a dot leader, a markdown table separator row, or a run of ㅋ the visitor asked for
- A markdown table, CSV, JSON dump or numbered list that runs out of budget is labelled 'The answer stopped at the length limit before it was finished.' (truncated = 'length', shown_chars = raw_chars) — never 'repetition', and never shortened
- The prompt is no longer auto-scored against an unrelated benchmark sample: '드' shows 'Free question — not auto-scored' instead of '✗ Wrong'

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/degenerate.ts detectDegenerate()/guardAnswer(); packages/node/test/degenerate.test.ts + fixtures/degenerate-corpus.json (293 real answers) + fixtures/degenerate-legit-adversarial.json (57 legitimate answers written to trip the guard)`
- `packages/node/src/runtime.ts chat()/completeDetailed() sampling + guard; DEFAULT_CHAT_SAMPLING stop ['\n\n\n\n','<think>']`
- `packages/web/src/components/chat/TurnView.tsx Truncation; packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts chat.trunc.*`

- Benchmark verification is unaffected: it still sends BenchmarkSpec.samples verbatim through /v1/completions with no stop sequences and no guard

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/components/chat/ChatComposer.tsx insert()/submit() (no trim), ␣ marker with aria-label unchanged`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts matchBenchmarkSample() (trimmed equality first, containment only ≥ 8 chars); packages/web/src/components/chat/util.ts matchSample mirror`
- `packages/node/src/runtime.ts verify() — sampling: null on every generation`

### AZ-225 - A question asked while another process holds the shared model is queued, not lost, and giving up costs nothing

**Goal:** When someone else — another visitor, a verifier, another node on the same machine — is using the shared model, the visitor is told they are in line, who is ahead of them and for how long, and can stop waiting without spending a free try.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Model server available; krx-all-2761 testable on node-a
- A SECOND process holds the cross-process runtime lease of the same patch mailbox (<runtime.patchDir>/.ainize-runtime.lock with a live holder pid) — the e2e helper holdRuntimeLock() takes it exactly the way a node does

**Steps**

1. Run one live test on http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761 so the free-trial counter shows a number
2. From another process, take the shared runtime lease on the same mailbox
3. Read the knowledge picker
4. Ask a question while the lease is held
5. Press 'Stop waiting' and read the free-trial counter
6. Release the lease and ask the same question again

**Expected**

- The picker says 'Someone else is testing on the shared model right now.' with 'Another test in progress (chat:krx-all-2761, node process <pid>) — started {n}s ago' and the one-at-a-time explanation — naming the FOREIGN holder, never 'Your test has the shared model' (that line is for this tab's own in-flight request only)
- The transcript's own pending turn says 'Queued behind another test — your question has not been lost.' within a couple of seconds, names the holder ('Someone else has the shared model (chat:krx-all-2761, started {n}s ago).') and shows a ticking 'waiting {n}s' counter
- The queue position line is shown only when someone is ahead in this node's own queue (alone in line it stays quiet)
- The pending row's button reads 'Stop waiting' rather than 'Cancel'
- Pressing it returns {cancelled: true, reason: 'queued', charged: false}; the turn says 'You stopped waiting. The node had not started this test yet, so no free try was used.' and offers Retry
- The free-trial counter is unchanged — nothing was sent to the model, so nothing was charged (POST /api/chat answers HTTP 499)
- Once the holder lets go the banner disappears and the same question is answered normally, charging exactly one free try

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/chat-queue.ts (ticket registry: queued/running/gone, per-visitor, free cancel while queued); packages/node/src/runtime.ts lockHolder()/queueState()/serial() onEnter`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/chat/status, POST /api/chat/cancel, quota consumed only after a successful chat`
- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx lockIsMine={queue?.state === 'running'} (RTK Query keeps `data` after a query is skipped, so reading `qs` directly made every later holder read as 'your test'); packages/web/src/components/chat/TurnView.tsx QueuePending; packages/web/src/components/chat/util.ts lockKind()`

### AZ-226 - Ask a follow-up in Compare mode and confirm each column replays only its own earlier answers

**Goal:** The comparison stays a comparison past turn 1: the un-patched column is never told it produced the knowledge's answer, so the 'Before loading' column keeps being wrong and the visitor sees the real difference the knowledge makes.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- No operator session; quota not exhausted
- Model server available; krx-all-2761 testable on node-a
- Browser DevTools open on the Network panel

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761 in the default 'Compare' view
2. Click the chip '종목코드 픽셀플러스', press Enter and wait for both columns
3. Type '방금 말한 종목코드를 숫자만 다시 알려줘' and press Enter
4. Read both columns of the second turn and the line under the transcript
5. In DevTools open the second POST http://localhost:3402/api/chat request and read its JSON payload

**Expected**

- Turn 1: 'Before loading' answers with a wrong ticker (✗ Wrong, Expected: 087600) and 'After loading' with 087600 (✓ Correct)
- Turn 2's payload carries three arrays: `messages` and `messages_patched` replay the PATCHED answer of turn 1, `messages_base` replays the BASE answer of turn 1, and all three end with the same new question — POST /api/chat rejects (400) a `messages_base`/`messages_patched` whose last message differs from `messages`
- The response reports what was replayed: history {base: 3, patched: 3, split: true}
- Turn 2's 'Before loading' column repeats its OWN wrong ticker and does not contain 087600 — the un-patched model was never shown the knowledge's answer
- Turn 2's 'After loading' column still answers 087600
- A line under the transcript says 'On follow-up questions each column replays only its own earlier answers — the "Before loading" model is never shown what the knowledge answered.' (ko: '이어지는 질문에서 각 열은 자기가 한 답만 다시 보게 됩니다 …'), shown in Compare mode once a turn has finished

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx buildHistory(prior, text, column) + the compare-mode split sent as messages_base / messages_patched`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/chat (messages_base / messages_patched, both refined to end with the same message as messages); packages/node/src/market.ts chatInner() msgsBase / msgsPatched and the reported `history``
- `packages/cli/src/commands/chat.ts chatRepl(): one transcript per column (state.transcript / state.baseTranscript)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts: chat.history.split`

### AZ-034 - Buy verified knowledge with the node's wallet from the Buy tab and load it from Purchased knowledge

**Goal:** An operator of another node can buy knowledge in one click from the web console and then load it into that node's model without touching the CLI.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- node-b web console http://localhost:3403 with its operator password set (create it on http://localhost:3403/signing if "Create the operator password" is shown) and signed in
- node-b holds at least 25 AIN (curl -s http://localhost:3403/api/chain shows balance; top up with `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js chain fund <node-b address> 30`)
- krx-all-2761 is LISTED (quorum 2/2) and has not been bought by node-b yet (GET http://localhost:3403/api/patches/krx-all-2761 shows purchased:false) — if it was already bought (CLI scenario), use node-c's console http://localhost:3404 instead
- node-b runtime available (GET http://localhost:3403/api/runtime available:true)
- node-a address = GET http://localhost:3402/api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3403/0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5/krx-all-2761 and click the "Buy" tab
2. Read the "Buy from this node" section
3. Click the button "Buy · 25 AIN" and wait
4. Read the green alert, the timeline and the key/value block under it
5. Reload the page and re-read the "Buy from this node" section
6. Open http://localhost:3403/dashboard and scroll to "Purchased knowledge"
7. Click "Load into model" on the krx-all-2761 row and wait; then click "Unload"

**Expected**

- "Buy from this node" shows the button "Buy · 25 AIN" with the note "Pays from this node’s AIN wallet and stores the file on this node. · AIN = AI Network token (this demo runs a local dev chain)" (no "Sign in as this node’s operator…" text)
- While paying the button reads "Paying…"; POST /api/patches/krx-all-2761/buy returns 200 (the web flow sends apply:false, so nothing is loaded yet)
- Green alert "Purchased krx-all-2761 · 25 AIN · tx 0x…" followed by "(ain-transfer)"; timeline rows in order "verification confirmed" (2 attestation(s) ≥ quorum 2), "price quoted" (Payment Required: 25 AIN → 0xF7A9dE499… (ain-transfer)), "paid" (AIN transfer tx 0x…), "settlement recorded" (seller confirmed; manifest sha256 …), "downloaded & verified" (body already present; sha256 matches on-ledger anchor — node-b holds it as a verifier), "manifest received" (on-chain access receipt written (/apps/knowledge/access/…, tx …)) — note the last label describes the on-chain access receipt, not the manifest; flag as a copy issue

Files are kept on this node; one click loads them into the model or takes them out.") has columns "Paid", "Payment record", "Downloaded file", "In model", "Load / unload" plus a Live test icon; the row shows the name link "KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)" with sub-line krx-all-2761, "25 AIN (wallet (AIN))", the shortened tx hash, "…/57c9346349afd6fa1b", "In model" = "no"

### AZ-035 - Reject invalid password setup input client-side and refuse a second setup server-side

**Goal:** A creator gets clear guidance when the setup form is filled wrongly and cannot be overwritten by a second setup.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** signing - **Automation:** e2e

- A node whose password is already set (http://localhost:3402 after C-01)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3403/signing
2. Type abc in "Password" and abc in "Confirm password", tick the terms box, click "Confirm"
3. Type abcd in "Password" and abce in "Confirm password", click "Confirm"
4. Type abcd in both fields, untick the terms box, click "Confirm"
5. Run: curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -X POST http://localhost:3402/api/auth/setup -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"password":"other123"}'

**Expected**

- Step 2 shows the error alert "Password must be at least 4 characters." and no request is sent
- Step 3 shows "Passwords do not match."
- Step 4 shows "Please agree to the Terms and Policies."
- Step 5 prints 409 and the response body error is "operator password already set"; the existing password still works

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/SigningPage.tsx onSetup`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/operator.ts op.sign.err.short, op.sign.err.mismatch, op.sign.err.terms`

4. Enter path /tmp/does-not-exist.npz and click "Save draft"

6. Set Id (optional) = A (single character) and click "Save draft"

### AZ-041 - Try Verify now on your own knowledge and be told why you cannot

**Goal:** An operator learns that a verification of their own knowledge would never count, before spending GPU minutes on it — the badge only means other nodes ran it.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** verification - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Signed in on http://localhost:3402 (node-a roles include verifier)
- The knowledge under test was published by node-a itself (AZ-031's visibility:test knowledge, or any id from GET /api/me/patches) — on this cluster node-a is the only seller, so every catalog entry is its own
- Shipped config: verifier.allowSelfAttest is false
- node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/project/0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5/<own knowledge id>
2. Look under 'Verification' for the 'Verify now (this node)' button
3. Read the line printed in its place
4. Call POST /api/patches/<id>/verify with the operator token directly (what `ainize patch verify` does)
5. Re-read the 'Verification' line and the 'Verification results' table

**Expected**

- No 'Verify now (this node)' button is rendered on your own knowledge (it is offered only for knowledge published by another node)
- In its place: 'You cannot verify your own knowledge. Verified means other nodes ran it on a real model — an attestation by this node would not count.'
- POST /api/patches/<id>/verify returns 409 {"error":"cannot verify your own knowledge: <id> was published by this node (verifier.allowSelfAttest is false). A self-check never counts toward the quorum — another node has to verify it."} and no attestation record is appended (GET /api/patches/<id> attestation count unchanged)
- `ainize patch verify <id>` prints the same sentence and exits non-zero
- The 'Verification' line still reads 'executed 2/2 passed · integrity 0 · 2 result(s)' — the numerator never exceeds the quorum, and a self-check would have been reported separately as 'n self-check(s), not counted' rather than counted

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/ManagePage.tsx canVerify (isMine), op.manage.self_verify`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/patches/:id/verify → Verifier.verifyOne`
- `packages/node/src/verifier.ts verifyOne (author check before any GPU work), packages/node/src/market.ts attest (write refused)`
- `packages/core/src/catalog.ts deriveCatalog (self_checks excluded from passed/status)`
- `packages/node/test/trust.test.ts item 146`

### AZ-046 - Remove and re-add a connected peer node

**Goal:** A creator controls which nodes their node connects to first, and the change persists in the node config.

4. Type http://localhost:3404/ (with trailing slash) into "Add node endpoint" and click "Add"
5. Wait ~30 s and re-read the "Last seen" cell of the re-added row
6. Open http://localhost:3402/network

**Expected**

- Description: "Nodes this one connects to first; the rest are discovered automatically. See the whole network on the Network page." (Network is a link to /network); columns "Endpoint", "Name", "Account address", "Roles", "Last seen", "Failures" plus an unlabeled 7th column holding the Remove button
- Rows show node-b (roles "verifier") and node-c (roles "verifier, serving") with a relative "Last seen" and Failures 0
- Remove: DELETE /api/peers returns 200 {ok:true}; green alert "Node removed."; the :3404 row disappears — but only briefly: node-c has node-a as a configured peer and calls /p2p/hello every ~4 s, and the peer exchange re-adds it, so the row re-appears automatically within seconds (auto-rediscovery is expected behaviour)
- Add: the trailing slash is stripped, POST /api/peers {endpoint:"http://localhost:3404"} returns 200; green alert "Node added."; the row is present with a fresh relative time (or "never" until the next hello)
- After a while Last seen updates to a recent "<n>s ago"
- Network page lists all three nodes again

**Evidence**

## Node operator / developer

### AZ-051 - Log in and out as operator from the CLI (first login sets the node password) and observe the 401 guard

**Goal:** An operator can obtain and drop a bearer session for operator-only commands without opening the browser.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** account - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running
- `curl -s http://localhost:3403/api/auth/me` returns `"needsSetup":true` for node-b (no operator password set yet) — otherwise the tester must know node-b's existing password
- Shell: `export PATH="$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH"; cd /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace; B="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-b"`
- node-b address = `curl -s http://localhost:3403/api/chain` (currently 0x430b3A373fa4FF685ca40d03BA455c26c0959935)

**Steps**

1. Run `$B wallet` before logging in
2. Run `$B login --password demo-pass-1234`
3. Run `curl -s http://localhost:3403/api/auth/me`
4. Run `$B wallet`
5. Run `$B logout`
6. Run `$B wallet` again
7. Run `$B login --password wrong-password`; then `echo $?`
8. Run `NGRAM_PASSWORD=demo-pass-1234 $B login` and inspect `$HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-b/cli.json` permissions with `stat -c %a`

**Expected**

- Step 1 fails with `error: operator login required — run \`ainize login\` first`; exit code 3
- Step 2 prints `✓ operator password set and logged in to http://localhost:3403 (token saved in /home/<user>/.ngram-cluster/node-b/cli.json)`
- Step 3 now returns `"signedIn":false,"needsSetup":false` (cookie-less curl is not signed in, but the password is set)
- Step 4 prints a key/value block padded to the widest key: `address             0x430b3A373fa4FF685ca40d03BA455c26c0959935`, `ledger              ain · ain:local`, `balance             <n> AIN`, then `sales`, `royalties received`, `purchases` counters
- Step 5 prints `✓ logged out`; cli.json no longer contains `token`
- Step 6 fails again with `error: operator login required — run \`ainize login\` first` (exit 3)
- Step 7 fails with `error: wrong password — run \`ainize login\` first` (every 401 gets the login hint) and exit code 3
- Step 8 prints `✓ logged in to http://localhost:3403 (token saved in …/cli.json)` (no 'operator password set and' prefix this time) and cli.json has mode 600

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/auth.ts (login/logout messages, NGRAM_PASSWORD)`
- `packages/cli/src/client.ts (401 → ' — run `ainize login` first', exit 3)`
- `packages/cli/src/context.ts writeState mode 0o600`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/branch.ts wallet() kv padding`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts /api/auth/me, /api/auth/setup, /api/auth/login, requireOperator`

### AZ-052 - Announce a public patch and watch node-b and node-c verify it on the real model until it is LISTED

**Goal:** A creator sees independent verifiers execute the benchmark on the serving model and list the knowledge once quorum is met, with no self-attestation.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** verification - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- node-a operator logged in from the CLI (`A="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a"; $A login --password <node-a password>`); `NPZ=/mnt/newdata/qwen3.8/results/train-fact/픽셀플러스.npz`; vLLM serving Qwen3.8-Flash-Next reachable at http://localhost:8000 (`$A status` shows `runtime     available · Qwen3.8-Flash-Next · hook ok`)
- Use a benchmark schema that no existing patch uses so that listing does not supersede the demo's krx-all-2761: `BENCH08='{"schema":"o08-pixel-check","queries":1,"format":["template"],"samples":[{"prompt":"종목코드 픽셀플러스 ","expect":"087600"}]}'`
- node-b home for logs: `B="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-b"`; verifier addresses from GET /api/nodes (currently node-b 0x430b3A37…, node-c 0x199Ff870…)
- Tester accepts a permanent extra public anchor on the local dev chain

**Steps**

1. Run `$A publish $NPZ --id o08-pixel-copy --name "[o08 test] Pixelplus ticker copy" --model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next --benchmark "$BENCH08" --price 0.1`
2. Immediately run `curl -si http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/o08-pixel-copy | head -1` and `curl -s http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/o08-pixel-copy`
3. Run `$B logs --follow` (node-b home, no --kind so both 'verifier' and 'verify' events show) in a second terminal
4. Poll `$A patch get o08-pixel-copy` every 20 s for up to 5 minutes
5. Run `$A patch ls --status LISTED`
6. Run `$A patch get krx-all-2761 | grep -i superseded`
7. Run `$A ledger ls --kind attest --limit 3`

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `✓ draft created: o08-pixel-copy  (2,992 rows, sha256 …)` and `✓ announced o08-pixel-copy → ledger record <hash> (verifiers will now attest; quorum lists it)`; `$A logs --kind publish --limit 2` shows `announced o08-pixel-copy (conflicts: N)`. N is every knowledge on this node whose address set overlaps the new body — 4 on a node holding only the demo bodies, and one more for each pixel copy earlier runs announced. The pre-check runs inside the node and counts private drafts (taught lessons) too, so it equals what GET /api/patches/:id/conflicts reports TO THE OPERATOR; a visitor is shown fewer, because private drafts are redacted from public overlap answers.
- Step 2 returns `HTTP/1.1 423 Locked` with body `{"error":"patch not listed yet (verification 0/2)"}` (or 1/2 if one verifier already finished)
- Step 3 shows on node-b, in order: `verifier` lines `verifying o08-pixel-copy ([o08 test] Pixelplus ticker copy)` and `benchmark o08-pixel-copy: 1/1 restarts=0`, then a `verify` line `attested o08-pixel-copy: PASS (vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next)`; node-c logs the same (they run sequentially because they share the runtime lock)
- Step 4: status goes ANNOUNCED → VERIFYING (after the first attestation) → LISTED with `verification 2/2 passed ✓ quorum`; the attestations table has exactly two rows, verifiers node-b and node-c, `PASS  free_generation=1/1 pre_apply=…  vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next`; node-a (the author) never appears as a verifier
- Step 5 lists both krx-all-2761 and o08-pixel-copy
- Step 6 prints nothing: krx-all-2761 is not superseded (different benchmark schema, so the overlap is not a supersede candidate)
- Step 7 shows the two new `attest` rows `o08-pixel-copy · PASS · vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` with AUTHOR node-b 0x430b3A… and node-c 0x199Ff8…

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/verifier.ts round()/verifyOne() (allowSelfAttest false, vllm:<model>, log messages)`
- `packages/node/src/runtime.ts verify() (free_generation score, restarts_detected)`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts announce() (pending_supersede only for same_schema), reconcileSupersedes(), attest() (kind 'verify')`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id 423 when !quorum_ok`
- `~/.ngram-cluster/node-a/config.json verifier.quorum=2, intervalMs=5000`

### AZ-053 - Use knowledge in one line: `ainize use krx-all-2761` verifies, pays in AIN, downloads and loads it; then re-run and remove

**Goal:** A node operator can acquire and activate verified knowledge with one command and see the money and state changes on every side.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running on the AIN ledger; node-b operator logged in (O-02); node-b balance ≥ 25 AIN (`curl -s http://localhost:3403/api/chain` shows balance)
- node-a operator logged in in another shell for the seller-side check (`A=...node-a`)
- Shell: `B="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-b"`; note node-b's balance before: `$B wallet | grep balance`
- Addresses: node-a 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5, node-b 0x430b3A373fa4FF685ca40d03BA455c26c0959935 (current; read from GET /api/nodes)

**Steps**

1. Run `$B use krx-all-2761`
2. Run `$B wallet`
3. Run `$A wallet` (node-a) and `$A ledger ls --kind settle --limit 3`
4. Run `$B patch get krx-all-2761 | head -1`
5. Run `$B chat krx-all-2761 --mode patched "종목코드 삼성전자"`
6. Run `$B use krx-all-2761` a second time
7. Run `$B patch remove krx-all-2761`
8. Run `$B patch ls --status LISTED`

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `✓ bought krx-all-2761 for 25 (ain-transfer)  tx 0x…` followed by `+<ms>ms`-prefixed step lines: `quorum    2 attestation(s) ≥ quorum 2`, `402       Payment Required: 25 AIN → 0xF7A9dE499… (ain-transfer)`, `pay       AIN transfer tx 0x…`, `settled   seller confirmed; manifest sha256 …`, `download  body already present; sha256 matches on-ledger anchor` (node-b already holds the body as a verifier), `receipt   on-chain access receipt written (/apps/knowledge/access/…, tx …)`, `apply     <scripts/patch.py output>`, `body: /home/<user>/.ngram-cluster/node-b/data/blobs/57c9346349afd6fa1b0475340b9b041ee0027753c05463d4bb204a5a4726c642.npz`, and finally `loaded into the model — try: ainize chat krx-all-2761 "your question"`
- Step 2 shows `balance` reduced by 25 AIN and `purchases    1`
- Step 3: node-a's wallet lists a `recent sales` row `krx-all-2761  25 AIN  0x430b3A37…9935  <time>`; `ledger ls --kind settle` shows `krx-all-2761 · 25 AIN · buyer 0x430b…9935`; `patch ls` SOLD column for krx-all-2761 increments by 1 on every node
- Step 4 header shows `LISTED  purchased  applied`
- Step 5 prints `after (krx-all-2761 loaded)  <n> ms · already loaded` with an answer containing 005930 and the footer `correct ✓ (benchmark)  model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` (no apply cost: the patch is already in the model)
- Step 6 prints `✓ krx-all-2761 is already on this node (purchased)`, `✓ applied krx-all-2761: …` and `try it: ainize chat krx-all-2761 "your question"` — no second payment, node-b balance unchanged
- Step 7 prints `✓ removed krx-all-2761: …`; `$B patch get krx-all-2761` no longer shows `applied`; `curl -s http://localhost:3403/api/runtime` has `"applied":[]`
- Step 8 still shows krx-all-2761 LISTED (buying does not change status)

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/patch.ts patchUse/patchBuy/patchApply/patchRemove output`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/chat.ts renderChat ('already loaded', 'correct ✓ (benchmark)')`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts buy() steps (quorum/402/pay/settled/download/receipt/apply), settlePayment() ain-transfer, isApplied()`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/patches/:id/buy, GET /api/me/wallet, GET /api/runtime`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/branch.ts wallet(); packages/cli/src/commands/ledger.ts summary()`

### AZ-054 - Live-test knowledge from the CLI: `chat --list`, one-shot compare, `--mode`, `--thinking`, `--json`, quota footer and the interactive REPL

**Goal:** A developer can measure before/after answers of the shared model for any knowledge held on a node, script it with JSON, and hold a multi-turn session.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running; runtime available; node-a home used (`A="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a"`); node-a holds all four bodies (seller)
- An empty home for the anonymous run: `mkdir -p /tmp/ainize-anon`

**Steps**

1. Run `$A chat`
2. Run `$A chat --list`
3. Run `$A chat pixelplus-087600 "종목코드 픽셀플러스"` (node-a logged in)
4. Run `$A chat pixelplus-087600 --mode base "종목코드 픽셀플러스"` and `$A chat pixelplus-087600 --mode patched "종목코드 픽셀플러스"`
5. Run `$A chat pixelplus-087600 --thinking --max-tokens 400 "픽셀플러스의 종목코드는?"`
6. Run `$A --json chat pixelplus-087600 "종목코드 픽셀플러스" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(sorted(d))"`
7. Run `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home /tmp/ainize-anon --node http://localhost:3402 chat pixelplus-087600 --mode base "종목코드 픽셀플러스"`
8. Run `$A chat krx-all-2761 --mode patched`; type `종목코드 삼성전자` Enter; type `/mode compare` Enter; `/help` Enter; `/reset` Enter; `/quit` Enter
9. Run `printf '종목코드 삼성전자\n/quit\n' | $A chat krx-all-2761 --mode patched`
10. Run `$A chat pixelplus-087600 "x" --max-tokens 5000`

**Expected**

- Step 1 fails with `error: patch id required — \`ainize chat --list\` shows what this node can test`
- Step 2 prints `runtime ready  model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next  live-apply hook on` and a table `ID NAME MODEL FACTS MEMORY ROWS VERIFIED TRY` with 4 rows, each `2/2 ✓` and TRY `"종목코드 픽셀플러스" → 087600`, then the hint `ainize chat <ID> "<question>"   or   ainize chat <ID>   for an interactive session`
- Step 3 prints a `before (base model)  <n> ms` block, an `after (pixelplus-087600 loaded)  <n> ms · loaded in <n> ms` block whose answer contains 087600, and the footer `correct ✓ (benchmark)  model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next`; no `free live tests left` text (operator is unlimited)
- Step 4: `--mode base` prints only the `before (base model)` block and no correct/wrong marker; `--mode patched` prints only the `after (…)` block plus the marker (`wrong ✗ (benchmark)` would appear on a miss; `(no benchmark sample for this question)` for a free question)
- Step 5 shows dimmed reasoning lines prefixed `┆` inside the answer blocks before the final content
- Step 6 prints `['applied', 'applied_ms', 'base', 'benchmark_hit', 'benchmark_hits', 'history', 'mode', 'model', 'patch_id', 'patch_ids', 'patched', 'quota_limit', 'remaining_quota', 'was_applied']` and nothing else on stdout — `history` is `{base, patched, split}`, how many messages each column was actually sent (split:false here, a single-turn call)
- Step 7 (anonymous) ends with `free live tests left this hour: 19` (or one less per prior anonymous call from this IP in the hour)
- Step 8: banner `live test of krx-all-2761 · mode patched · /quit to exit, /help for commands`, prompt `you> ` (in `compare` mode a second dim line says 'follow-ups: each column replays only its own earlier answers — the base model is never shown the patched one', because the REPL keeps one transcript per column); the answer block `after (krx-all-2761 loaded)` contains 005930 with `correct ✓ (benchmark)`; `/mode compare` → `mode → compare`; `/help` lists `/mode base|patched|compare  (now: compare)`, `/reset   forget the transcript`, `/quit    exit`; `/reset` → `transcript cleared`; `/quit` → `bye — 1 turn(s)`; exit 0
- Step 9 works non-interactively (no prompt echo) and ends with `bye — 1 turn(s)`; after every call the shared table is restored (`curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/runtime` shows `"applied":[]`)

### AZ-055 - Drive the public and operator HTTP API with curl from /api/openapi.json: catalog, detail, benchmarks, info, 401 guards, login token and settings

**Goal:** A developer integrating without the CLI can discover every endpoint from the node's own OpenAPI document and rely on documented status codes.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** api - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running with krx-all-2761 LISTED and the other three SUPERSEDED; node-b password set (O-02: demo-pass-1234)
- Shell: `N=http://localhost:3402; NB=http://localhost:3403`
- node-b address = `curl -s $NB/api/chain` (currently 0x430b3A373fa4FF685ca40d03BA455c26c0959935)

**Steps**

1. Run `curl -s $N/api/openapi.json | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['openapi'], d['info']['title'], d['servers'], len(d['paths']), '/x402/patch/{id}' in d['paths'], '/api/chat' in d['paths'])"`
2. Run `curl -s $N/api/docs | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(sorted(d), d['cli']['oneLiners']['use']['en'])"`
3. Run `curl -s "$N/api/catalog?status=LISTED,SUPERSEDED&sort=price" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['total'], d['items'][0]['anchor']['id'], 'sig' in d['items'][0]['attestations'][0], d['models'], d['schemas'])"`
4. Run `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' "$N/api/catalog?limit=0"` and `curl -s "$N/api/catalog?sort=bogus"`
5. Run `curl -s $N/api/patches/krx-all-2761 | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['status'], d['quorum_ok'], d['lineage']['parents'][0]['id'], d['supersedes'], d['gateway_url'], d['has_body'], d['branches'][0]['name'])"`
6. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' $N/api/patches/does-not-exist` and `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' $N/api/benchmarks/none`
7. Run `curl -s $N/api/benchmarks/krx-ticker-codes | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(len(json.load(sys.stdin)['items']))"` and `curl -s $N/api/info | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['quorum'], d['currency'], d['counts'], d['peers'])"`
8. For each of `GET /api/me/wallet`, `GET /api/me/settings`, `POST /api/patches`, `POST /api/branches`, `POST /api/patches/krx-all-2761/buy`, `DELETE /api/peers` run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X <METHOD> $NB<path>` without credentials
9. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X POST $NB/api/auth/login -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"password":"wrong"}'`
10. Run `TOKEN=$(curl -s -X POST $NB/api/auth/login -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"password":"demo-pass-1234"}' | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['token'])")` then `curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" $NB/api/me/wallet | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(sorted(json.load(sys.stdin)))"`
11. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X POST $NB/api/auth/setup -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"password":"another-1234"}'`
12. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X PATCH $NB/api/me/settings -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"notifications":"weird"}'`; then `-d '{"notifications":"sales"}'`; then `-d '{"notifications":"all"}'` to restore
13. Run `curl -si $N/api/info | grep -i access-control-allow-origin`

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `3.1.0 Ainize node API [{'url': 'http://localhost:3402'}] 89 True True` — 89 documented paths, 55 marketplace + 34 teach-mode (23 of teach mode v1 + 11 dataset routes). The two D3 live-test queue endpoints (/api/chat/status, /api/chat/cancel) and the two health probes (/healthz, /readyz) are part of the 55.
- Step 2 prints `['cli', 'node', 'openapi'] Use knowledge (one line)`
- Step 3 prints `4 pixelplus-087600 False ['Qwen3.8-Flash-Next'] ['krx-ticker-codes']` (attestation signatures are stripped from the catalog)
- Step 4: both return 400 with body `{"error":"invalid request","issues":[…]}`
- Step 5 prints `LISTED True krx-all-2761-ep12 ['krx-all-2761-ep12', 'krx-all-2761-ep6', 'pixelplus-087600'] http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761 True finance/KRX-latest`
- Step 6 prints `{"error":"patch not found"} 404` and `{"error":"no patches for that benchmark schema"} 404`
- Step 7 prints `4` and `2 AIN {'patches': 4, 'listed': 1, 'verifying': 0, 'superseded': 3, 'rejected': 0} 2`
- Step 8: every call returns `{"error":"operator login required"} 401`
- Step 9 returns `{"error":"wrong password"} 401`
- Step 10 prints `['address', 'app', 'balance', 'height', 'kind', 'network', 'provider', 'purchases', 'records', 'royalties', 'sales', 'valid']` (200 with bearer auth; the login response also set an httpOnly `ngram_session` cookie)
- Step 11 returns `{"error":"operator password already set"} 409`
- Step 12: `weird` → 400 `invalid request`; `sales` → 200 `{"settings":{"notifications":"sales","display_name":"node-b","payout_address":"0x430b3A373fa4FF685ca40d03BA455c26c0959935"}}`; final call restores `all`
- Step 13 shows `access-control-allow-origin: *`

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts /api/openapi.json, /api/docs, /api/catalog (zod, sig stripped), /api/patches/:id, /api/benchmarks/:schema, /api/info, requireOperator 401, /api/auth/login 401, /api/auth/setup 409, /api/me/settings zod enum, error handler 400 'invalid request'`
