### AZ-056 - Probe the seller gateway's X-PAYMENT validation, the 423 not-listed state and the gated blob download with curl

**Goal:** A developer building a buyer can rely on the seller's exact payload error codes, the not-yet-listed response and the download gating (token or settled buyer only).

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running on the AIN ledger; `N=http://localhost:3402; SHA=57c9346349afd6fa1b0475340b9b041ee0027753c05463d4bb204a5a4726c642` (krx-all-2761 body)
- krx-all-2761 LISTED (2/2); for the 423 step an item that is still ANNOUNCED/VERIFYING (right after a fresh cluster start, or the o08-pixel-copy publish of the announce scenario)
- For the last step: node-b has purchased krx-all-2761 (one-line `use` scenario) and `TOKEN` for node-b is available (login via POST /api/auth/login, or `~/.ngram-cluster/node-b/cli.json`)
- The 402 handshake itself (headers, nonce, 409/404) and forged/replayed proofs are covered by the agent scenarios

**Steps**

1. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -H 'x-payment: not-base64-json' $N/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`
2. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -H "x-payment: $(echo -n '{"scheme":"paypal"}' | base64 -w0)" $N/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`
3. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -H "x-payment: $(echo -n '{"scheme":"ain-transfer"}' | base64 -w0)" $N/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`
4. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -H "x-payment: $(echo -n '{"scheme":"local-credit","nonce":"deadbeef","from":"0x430b3A373fa4FF685ca40d03BA455c26c0959935","proof":"00"}' | base64 -w0)" $N/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`
5. While an item is still VERIFYING run `curl -si $N/x402/patch/<that id> | sed -n '1p;$p'`
6. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -o /dev/null $N/p2p/blob/$SHA`
7. Run `DL=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" http://localhost:3403/api/me/purchases | python3 -c "import sys,json; print([p for p in json.load(sys.stdin)['items'] if p['patch_id']=='krx-all-2761'][0]['manifest']['download_token'])")` then `curl -sI "$N/p2p/blob/$SHA?token=$DL" | sed -n '1p;/content-type/p;/x-content-sha256/p;/content-disposition/p'`

**Expected**

- Step 1 returns `{"error":"missing or malformed X-PAYMENT"} 402`
- Step 2 returns `{"error":"unsupported scheme paypal"} 402`
- Step 3 returns `{"error":"ain-transfer payload needs txHash"} 402`
- Step 4 returns `{"error":"unknown or expired nonce"} 402` (local-credit is not the seller's scheme on the AIN ledger, but the single-use nonce check runs first; nonces expire after 10 minutes)
- Step 5 returns `HTTP/1.1 423 Locked` with `{"error":"patch not listed yet (verification <passed>/2)"}` — no price quote before quorum
- Step 6 returns 402 (`payment required: buy the patch via /x402/patch/:id (verifiers and authors are exempt)`)
- Step 7 prints `HTTP/1.1 200 OK`, `content-type: application/octet-stream`, `x-content-sha256: 57c93463…6c642` (equal to $SHA) and `content-disposition: attachment; filename="57c93463….npz"` — the manifest's download_token unlocks the body for 24 h; no settle record is written by any of the failing steps (`ledger ls --kind settle` count unchanged)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id (402/423 branches), GET /p2p/blob/:sha (402 message, headers)`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts settlePayment() error strings ('missing or malformed X-PAYMENT', 'unsupported scheme', 'ain-transfer payload needs txHash', 'unknown or expired nonce'), issueManifest() download_token 24h, mayDownload()`
- `packages/node/src/store.ts nonces takeNonce`
- `packages/core/src/x402.ts header names`

### AZ-057 - Bring up a fourth node with `ainize init`, fund it on the local AIN chain, start it detached, peer it with the demo cluster and stop it

**Goal:** An operator can create a node identity, join the existing 3-node network and control its lifecycle from the CLI without touching the web UI.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** cli - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running (node-a :3402, node-b :3403, node-c :3404) on the local AIN chain (docker container ngram-ain SERVING on :8081); demo patches already LISTED/SUPERSEDED
- Shell prepared: `export PATH="$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH"; cd /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace; H=$HOME/.ngram-o01; D="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $H"`
- $H does not exist yet

**Steps**

1. Run `$D init --name node-d --port 3410 --ledger ain --ain-provider http://localhost:8081 --peer http://localhost:3402 --roles verifier --public-url http://localhost:3410`
2. Run `$D init --name node-d --port 3410` a second time (no --force)
3. Run `$D keys show` and copy the printed address (ADDR)
4. Run `$D chain fund $ADDR 100`
5. Run `$D start -d`
6. Run `$D start -d` again while the node is up
7. Wait ~10 s, then run `$D status`
8. Run `$D peers ls`
9. Run `curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/nodes | python3 -c "import sys,json; print([n['name'] for n in json.load(sys.stdin)['nodes']])"`
10. Run `$D stop`, then `$D stop` once more
11. Cleanup: `rm -rf $H`

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `✓ node initialised at $H/config.json` with `name node-d`, `address 0x…` (20-byte hex), `port 3410`, `ledger ain`, `roles verifier`, followed by `next: \`ainize start\`   (then \`ainize login\`, \`ainize seed\`)`; exit code 0
- Step 2 fails with `error: config already exists at $H/config.json (use --force to overwrite, or \`ainize config show\`)`; exit code 1; config.json unchanged
- Step 3 prints `address` and `public key` lines plus the dim hint `add --reveal to print the private key`; no private key is shown
- Step 4 prints `✓ funded 0x… with 100 AIN  tx 0x…  balance now 100 AIN`
- Step 5 prints `✓ node started in the background (pid N) — port 3410` and `logs: $H/node.log   stop: ainize stop`; $H/node.pid contains N
- Step 6 fails with `error: node already running in the background (pid N) — \`ainize stop\` first`; exit code 1
- Step 7 shows `node-d  http://localhost:3410  (pid N)`, `roles       verifier`, `ledger      ain · ain:local · http://localhost:8081 · <n> records · height <h>` (same record count as `curl http://localhost:3402/api/info`), `peers       3` (the first gossip round's peer exchange with node-a added node-b and node-c — `peers 1` is only visible in the first second), `patches     4 (1 listed)`, `quorum      2`, `currency    AIN`
- Step 8 lists three rows: `http://localhost:3402  node-a  0xF7A9dE49…A1F5  seller,verifier,serving  <timestamp>  0` plus http://localhost:3403 and http://localhost:3404 (shown as `(unreached)` until their first hello succeeds)
- Step 9 output contains `node-d` (learned through /p2p/hello and the on-chain `node` record) next to node-a, node-b, node-c
- Step 10 first prints `✓ stopped node (pid N)`; the second call prints `no background node running for this NGRAM_HOME`; $H/node.pid is removed; port 3410 no longer answers

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/bin.ts (init/start/stop/status/keys/chain fund commands)`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/init.ts (init, requireConfig, keysShow messages)`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/node.ts (start/stop/status output, node.pid)`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/chain.ts (chainFund message)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/nodes, POST /p2p/hello; packages/node/src/p2p.ts peer exchange`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts registerSelf()`

### AZ-058 - Read node events with `ainize logs` filters and confirm `ainize seed` refuses to run against a live node

**Goal:** An operator can audit what a node did (verification, trades, p2p) from the terminal and cannot corrupt a running node's data directory by seeding.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** logs - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running; at least one purchase or verification has happened (default seeded state has attest events)
- Shell: `export PATH="$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH"; cd /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace; A="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a"; B="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-b"`

**Steps**

1. Run `$A seed`
2. Run `$B logs --kind verify --limit 20`
3. Run `$A logs --patch krx-all-2761 --limit 20`
4. Run `$A logs --limit 3 --json`
5. Run `$A logs --follow` in a second terminal, then in the first terminal run `curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3402/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"patch_id":"pixelplus-087600","mode":"base","max_tokens":4,"messages":[{"role":"user","content":"hi"}]}'`; then Ctrl+C the follower
6. Run `$A logs --kind nosuchkind`
7. Run `$A logs --kind patch --limit 5` from the logged-in operator home, then the same command from a home with no operator token (`--node http://localhost:3402 --home <empty home>`)
8. Run `$A logs --level warn --limit 5` and `$A logs --kind challenge`

**Expected**

- Step 1 fails with `error: a node is running at http://localhost:3402; seeding writes to its data directory — stop it first (\`ainize stop\`) or seed from the web console`; exit 1; catalog unchanged
- Step 2 prints lines formatted `<YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS> info  verify    [krx-all-2761] attested krx-all-2761: PASS (vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next)` (kind column padded to 9, patch id in brackets)
- Step 3 shows only events whose patch id is krx-all-2761 (e.g. `verifier`, `verify`, `publish`, `trade`, `usage` kinds)
- Step 4 prints a JSON array of at most 3 objects with keys `seq, ts, level, kind, patch_id, message, data`
- Step 5: within ~2 s the follower prints a new line `usage     [pixelplus-087600] live test pixelplus-087600 (base) by ip:::ffff:127.0.0.1: base only` (visitor key 'ip:' + req.ip, truncated to 24 chars); Ctrl+C exits cleanly
- Step 6 fails with `error: Invalid values:` / `  Argument: kind, Given: "nosuchkind", Choices: "blob", "branch", "buy", "challenge", "config", "drive", "node", "p2p", "patch", "payout", "publish", "runtime", "seed", "settings", "teach", "trade", "usage", "verifier", "verify"`; exit 1 — an unrecognised kind is never answered with an empty screen
- Step 7: the operator's terminal shows `patch` events including `draft created: …` lines (the CLI sends its operator token); the same command with no token prints `(no events match kind 'patch' — and you are not logged in, so teach and draft lines are hidden; run `ainize login`)` — the visitor stream drops every `draft …` line by design
- Step 8: `--level warn` prints only `warn` and `error` rows (the level is a floor, not an exact match); a filter that matches nothing prints `(no events match kind 'challenge')`, never a blank line

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/node.ts logs()/seed()`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/events, GET /api/patches/:id/events`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts log() kinds (verify, usage, trade, buy, publish)`

### AZ-059 - Add, list and remove peers on a node and watch gossip discover the other nodes

**Goal:** An operator can wire a node into the P2P mesh and prune it, with the change persisted to config.json.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** network - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- node-d from the fourth-node scenario running detached on :3410 with only node-a as seed peer (`H=$HOME/.ngram-o01; D="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $H"`); `$D login --password demo-pass-1234` done (sets node-d's operator password); run the steps right after start, before gossip adds the other peers
- Demo cluster running; node-b address from `$D nodes` (currently 0x430b3A37…9935)

2. Run `$D peers add localhost:3403`
3. Run `$D peers add http://localhost:3403/`
4. Wait ~8 s (two gossip ticks) and run `$D peers ls`
5. Run `$D config show | grep -A3 '"peers"'`
6. Run `$D nodes`
7. Run `$D peers rm http://localhost:3403`, wait ~8 s, run `$D peers ls`
8. Run `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -X POST http://localhost:3410/api/peers -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"endpoint":"http://localhost:3404"}'` (no auth; any demo node such as http://localhost:3403/api/peers behaves the same)

**Expected**

- Step 1 shows the seed peer row `http://localhost:3402  node-a …` (plus :3403/:3404 if gossip already ran)
- Step 2 fails with `error: endpoint must be an http(s) URL`
- Step 3 prints `✓ peer added: http://localhost:3403/` (echoed as typed; the trailing slash is stripped in the request)
- Step 4 lists both `http://localhost:3402 node-a` and `http://localhost:3403 node-b  0x430b3A37…9935  verifier` with a fresh LAST SEEN and FAILURES 0 (NAME is `(unreached)` only until the first hello succeeds)
- Step 5 shows `"peers": ["http://localhost:3402", "http://localhost:3403"]` persisted
- Step 6 prints `known nodes` (columns NAME ADDRESS ENDPOINT ROLES LEDGER BRANCHES BLOBS LAST SEEN; node-a, node-b, node-c, `node-d (self)`) and `configured peers` (ENDPOINT ADDRESS LAST SEEN FAILURES) tables
- Step 7 prints `✓ peer removed: http://localhost:3403`; afterwards `peers ls` no longer lists :3403 unless the peer exchange re-learns it, while :3402 remains
- Step 8 returns 401 (`{"error":"operator login required"}`) — peer management needs the operator session

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/peers.ts (peersLs/peersAdd/peersRm messages)`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/node.ts nodesTable()`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST/DELETE /api/peers (saveConfig), GET /api/nodes, POST /p2p/hello`

- Step 2: the first call lists only krx-all-2761; the second only pixelplus-087600
- Step 3 prints `['pixelplus-087600', 'krx-all-2761-ep6', 'krx-all-2761-ep12', 'krx-all-2761']` (ascending price)
- Step 4 shows the bold name + `LISTED  (yours)`, key/values `id krx-all-2761`, `price 25 AIN · per_download`, `benchmark krx-ticker-codes · 2761 queries · template/chat · collateral ≤ 0.08 nat`, `gateway http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`, `verification 2/2 passed ✓ quorum`, `body on this node yes`; an `attestations` table (VERIFIER RESULT SCORE VERIFIED ON RESTARTS COUNTS AT) with two rows (node-b, node-c) `PASS  free_generation=26/26 pre_apply=1/8  vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next  0  yes` — the STAKE column is gone because no deposit was ever escrowed (critique 2 item 127), and COUNTS says whether the result counted toward the quorum (`no — self-check` for an attestation by the anchor's own author); `lineage` with `parents : krx-all-2761-ep12 (SUPERSEDED)` and `supersedes: krx-all-2761-ep12, krx-all-2761-ep6, pixelplus-087600`; an `address-set overlaps (A₁ ∩ A₂)` table; `branches` listing `finance/KRX-latest {"market":"KRX","version":"latest"}`
- Step 5 shows `SUPERSEDED` and the warning line `superseded by: krx-all-2761`
- Step 6 prints three rows: `krx-all-2761-ep12 241,992 yes SUPERSEDED`, `krx-all-2761-ep6 241,992 yes SUPERSEDED`, `pixelplus-087600 2,170 yes SUPERSEDED`
- Step 7 lists ledger records of kinds `anchor` (author node-a 0xF7A9…), two `attest` (authors node-b 0x430b3A… and node-c 0x199Ff8…) and three `supersede` rows with a HASH column
- Step 8 fails with `error: patch not found`; exit 1

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/patch.ts patchLs/patchGet/patchConflicts/patchRecords`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/catalog, /api/patches/:id, /api/patches/:id/records, /api/patches/:id/conflicts`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts conflicts()`

### AZ-061 - Register a draft with `ainize publish --no-announce`, check its visibility, reject bad inputs and delete it

**Goal:** A creator can stage knowledge locally, see overlaps before anything reaches the ledger, and clean up without leaving traces.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** new-patch - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running; node-a operator logged in from the CLI (`$A login --password <node-a password>`; node-a's password is the one set on first visit to http://localhost:3402/signing, or set it now if `/api/auth/me` says needsSetup)
- Real body file exists on the node machine: /mnt/newdata/qwen3.8/results/train-fact/픽셀플러스.npz
- Shell: `export PATH="$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH"; cd /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace; A="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a"; NPZ=/mnt/newdata/qwen3.8/results/train-fact/픽셀플러스.npz; BENCH='{"schema":"krx-ticker-codes","queries":1,"format":["template"],"samples":[{"prompt":"종목코드 픽셀플러스 ","expect":"087600"}]}'`

**Steps**

1. Run `$A publish ./missing.npz --name x --model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next --benchmark "$BENCH"`
2. Run `$A publish README.md --name x --model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next --benchmark "$BENCH"`
3. Run `$A publish $NPZ --name x --model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next --benchmark '{"queries":1}'`
4. Run `$A publish $NPZ --id o06-draft --name "O06 draft test" --model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next --benchmark "$BENCH" --price 0.1 --no-announce`
5. Run `$A patch ls` and `$A patch ls --drafts`
6. Run `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' http://localhost:3402/api/patches/o06-draft`
7. Run `$A patch conflicts o06-draft`
8. Run `$A use o06-draft`
9. Run `$A patch rm o06-draft` then `$A patch ls --drafts`

**Expected**

- Step 1 fails with `error: file not found: /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace/missing.npz`
- Step 2 fails with `error: patch body must be a .npz (addrs/before/after arrays)`
- Step 3 fails with `error: benchmark.schema is required (e.g. "krx-ticker-codes")`
- Step 4 prints `✓ draft created: o06-draft  (2,992 rows, sha256 62d0978ccbf0…)` followed by `announce when ready: ainize patch announce o06-draft`; no ledger record is written (`$A ledger ls --kind anchor` count unchanged)
- Step 5: plain `patch ls` does not contain o06-draft; `--drafts` shows `o06-draft  DRAFT  node-a … 2,992  3.7 MB  0.1 AIN  0/2`
- Step 6 returns 404 (drafts are invisible to non-operators)
- Step 7 lists `pixelplus-087600  2,992  yes  SUPERSEDED` (identical body → full overlap) and `krx-all-2761  2,170  yes  LISTED`, plus rows for krx-all-2761-ep12 and krx-all-2761-ep6 (node-a holds all four bodies; counts from live data)
- Step 8 fails with `error: o06-draft is DRAFT (verification 0/2) — not verified yet; try \`ainize patch get o06-draft\``
- Step 9 prints `✓ draft o06-draft deleted`; `--drafts` no longer lists it

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/patch.ts patchPublish/patchRm/patchUse validation messages`
- `packages/cli/src/bin.ts publish one-liner (--announce default true, --no-announce)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/patches (path on node), GET /api/patches/:id (DRAFT → 404 for non-operator), DELETE /api/patches/:id`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts createDraft(), conflicts()`

### AZ-062 - Use a SUPERSEDED knowledge with `ainize use --no-apply` and get the newer-version note

**Goal:** A buyer of a point-in-time version is warned that a newer version exists but can still download it without loading it into the model.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- node-b operator logged in with ≥ 0.1 AIN (O-02); pixelplus-087600 not yet purchased by node-b (`$B patch get pixelplus-087600 | head -1` shows no `purchased`)
- node-b already holds the pixelplus body (verifier), so no download happens

**Steps**

1. Run `$B patch get pixelplus-087600 | grep -E 'SUPERSEDED|superseded by'`
2. Run `$B use pixelplus-087600 --no-apply`
3. Run `$B patch get pixelplus-087600 | head -1`
4. Run `curl -s http://localhost:3403/api/runtime | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['applied'])"`
5. Run `$B patch apply pixelplus-087600` then `$B patch remove pixelplus-087600`
6. Run `curl -s 'http://localhost:3402/api/catalog?status=SUPERSEDED' | python3 -c "import sys,json; print([(e['anchor']['id'], e['downloads']) for e in json.load(sys.stdin)['items']])"`

**Expected**

- Step 1 shows the status `SUPERSEDED` and the warning `superseded by: krx-all-2761` (it may list every newer same-subject version, e.g. `krx-all-2761, krx-all-2761-ep12, krx-all-2761-ep6` — match on the prefix)
- Step 2 first prints the dim note `note: a newer version exists on the same subject → krx-all-2761 … (newer version available)`, then `✓ bought pixelplus-087600 for 0.1 (ain-transfer)  tx 0x…` with steps quorum/402 (`0.1 AIN`)/pay/settled/download (`body already present; sha256 matches on-ledger anchor`)/receipt but no `apply` step, and ends with `downloaded — load with: ainize patch apply pixelplus-087600`
- Step 3 header shows `SUPERSEDED  purchased` (not `applied`)
- Step 4 prints `[]` — nothing was loaded into the shared model
- Step 5 prints `✓ applied pixelplus-087600: …` then `✓ removed pixelplus-087600: …`
- Step 6 shows pixelplus-087600 with downloads incremented by 1 — SUPERSEDED knowledge remains purchasable

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/patch.ts patchUse (SUPERSEDED accepted; superseded_by note; --no-apply hint)`
- `packages/cli/src/bin.ts use command (--apply default true)`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts buy() (quorum_ok is the only gate; status not checked)`

### AZ-063 - Audit the shared ledger with `ledger ls`, `ledger verify`, `ledger graph` and `ledger export`, and cross-check two nodes

**Goal:** An operator can prove that provenance records are intact and identical across nodes and take a machine-readable copy.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** ledger - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running on the AIN ledger (record count is a live value: 10 right after a fresh start, ~20 once attestations/supersedes landed)
- Shell: `A="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a"; OUT=/tmp/ainize-ledger.jsonl; mkdir -p /tmp/ainize-anon`

**Steps**

1. Run `$A ledger ls --limit 10`
2. Run `$A ledger ls --kind attest` and `$A ledger ls --kind supersede`
3. Run `$A ledger verify` and `curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/ledger/verify`
4. Run `$A ledger graph`
5. Run `$A ledger export $OUT`; then `wc -l $OUT` and `head -1 $OUT | python3 -c "import sys,json; r=json.load(sys.stdin); print(sorted(r)[:6], r['kind'])"`
6. Run `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --node http://localhost:3403 --home /tmp/ainize-anon ledger ls --limit 1 | head -3` and compare the `records` count with node-a
7. Run `$A ledger ls --kind bogus`, then `$A ledger ls --kind subscribe --limit 1` and `$A --node localhost:3402 status`

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `ledger   ain · ain:local · http://localhost:8081`, `records  <n>`, `height   <h>`, `head     -` and a table `AT KIND AUTHOR SUMMARY HASH`
- Step 2: attest rows read `krx-all-2761 · PASS · vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` (one per verifier per patch); supersede rows read `krx-all-2761 supersedes pixelplus-087600 (2170 rows)`, `krx-all-2761 supersedes krx-all-2761-ep6 (241992 rows)`, `krx-all-2761 supersedes krx-all-2761-ep12 (241992 rows)`
- Step 3 prints `✓ ledger valid — <n> record(s) checked (hashes, signatures, imported chain linkage)`; the API returns `{"valid":true,"checked":<n>,"errors":[]}` with the same n
- Step 4 prints `lineage (child → parent edges, royalties flow upward)` and the tree `krx-all-2761-ep6 [Qwen3.8-Flash-Next · krx-ticker-codes] SUPERSEDED` → `└─ krx-all-2761-ep12 … SUPERSEDED` → (indented one level further) `└─ krx-all-2761 … LISTED  supersedes krx-all-2761-ep12, krx-all-2761-ep6, pixelplus-087600`, plus a separate root `pixelplus-087600 … SUPERSEDED`
- Step 5 prints `✓ exported <n> record(s) to /tmp/ainize-ledger.jsonl` where n is the node's whole `records` count — the CLI asks how big the ledger is and requests exactly that many (it used to ask for a fixed 1,000 and printed 'exported 1000 record(s)' once the chain grew past it). Past the node's per-request maximum (5,000) it says so instead: `exported the most recent 5000 of <n> record(s) … cannot page further back`. `wc -l` equals n; each line is one JSON record whose keys are `author, body, hash, kind, parents, sig, ts` (sorted(r)[:6] → ['author','body','hash','kind','parents','sig']), oldest first
- Step 6: node-b reports the same `records` count and network `ain:local` — one shared chain, no divergence
- Step 7 fails with `error: Invalid values:` / `  Argument: kind, Given: "bogus", Choices: "anchor", "attest", "settle", "challenge", "branch", "node", "supersede", "subscribe"`, exit 1 — an unrecognised kind is never answered with `ledger is empty` two lines under a four-figure record count. A kind that is real but matches nothing prints `no records of kind 'subscribe' (<n> record(s) in the ledger)`. And `--node localhost:3402` — a scheme-less URL — fails with `error: --node must be a full URL — did you mean http://localhost:3402?` (exit 2) instead of reporting a healthy node as unreachable

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/ledger.ts ledgerLs/ledgerVerify/ledgerGraph/ledgerExport (exact strings)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/ledger, /api/ledger/verify, /api/ledger/graph`
- `Live probe: /api/ledger/verify → {valid:true, checked:<n>, errors:[]}`

### AZ-064 - Create a branch, add knowledge, subscribe a node and route `jurisdiction=KR` to it

**Goal:** An operator can group knowledge into a context-tagged branch and have the gateway resolve a request context to the serving nodes that subscribe to it.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** network - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- node-a operator logged in (`A=…node-a`), runtime available; branch `law/KR` does not exist yet (`$A branch ls` shows only the two seeded tracks finance/KRX-latest and finance/KRX-history)
- node-b operator logged in (`B=…node-b`) for the ownership check
- node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)
- Tester accepts permanent branch/subscribe records on the local dev chain

**Steps**

1. Run `$A route jurisdiction=KR`
2. Run `$A branch create 'bad name!' --context jurisdiction=KR`
3. Run `$A branch create law/KR --description "O14 test branch" --context jurisdiction=KR`
4. Run `$A branch add law/KR pixelplus-087600`
5. Run `$B branch add law/KR krx-all-2761` (node-b is not the owner)
6. Run `$A route jurisdiction=KR`
7. Run `$A branch subscribe law/KR`
8. Run `$A route jurisdiction=KR` and `$A branch ls`
9. Run `$B branch ls` (node-b view) and `$A status | grep branches`
10. Run `$A route jurisdiction=US` and `$A route jurisdiction`
11. Run `$A branch unsubscribe law/KR` then `$A route jurisdiction=KR`

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `no branch matches {"jurisdiction":"KR"}` (the seeded tracks only carry market/version)
- Step 2 fails with `error: invalid branch name`
- Step 3 prints `✓ branch law/KR created {"jurisdiction":"KR"} with 0 patch(es)`; `$A ledger ls --kind branch --limit 1` shows `law/KR · 0 patch(es)`
- Step 4 prints `✓ pixelplus-087600 added to law/KR (1 patches)`
- Step 5 fails with `error: only the branch owner can add patches`
- Step 6 prints `context {"jurisdiction":"KR"} → branch law/KR {"jurisdiction":"KR"}` followed by `no node currently subscribes to that branch`
- Step 7 prints `✓ subscribed law/KR  (patches acquired and applied when a runtime is available)`; `$A logs --kind runtime --limit 2` shows `applied pixelplus-087600: …` (node-a authored it, so no purchase was needed)
- Step 8: route now shows a table `SERVING NODE node-a  ENDPOINT http://localhost:3402  MODEL Qwen3.8-Flash-Next  ADDRESS 0xF7A9dE49…A1F5`; `branch ls` shows three rows including `law/KR ✓` with CONTEXT `jurisdiction=KR`, PATCHES `pixelplus-087600`, SUBSCRIBERS `node-a`, and the legend `✓ = this node subscribes`
- Step 9: node-b's `branch ls` lists law/KR with SUBSCRIBERS `node-a` (without the ✓); node-a `status` prints `branches    law/KR`
- Step 10: `jurisdiction=US` → `no branch matches {"jurisdiction":"US"}`; bare `jurisdiction` → `error: context must be key=value, got "jurisdiction"`
- Step 11 prints `✓ unsubscribed law/KR`, pixelplus-087600 is removed from the runtime (`/api/runtime` applied is `[]`), and route again says `no node currently subscribes to that branch`

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/branch.ts branchCreate/branchAdd/branchSubscribe/route/parseContext strings`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts createBranch() name regex, addToBranch() owner check, subscribe() apply/remove, route()`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts /api/branches, /api/route, /api/branches/:name/subscribe`

### AZ-066 - Exhaust the anonymous live-test quota (20/hour per IP) via POST /api/chat and confirm operators are unmetered and failed calls are not charged

**Goal:** An operator can verify that free trials are capped per visitor while a logged-in operator and error responses do not consume tries.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** api - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running; no other anonymous live test from this machine's IP in the last hour (otherwise the counter starts lower)
- Operator bearer TOKEN for node-a (`curl -X POST http://localhost:3402/api/auth/login …` with node-a's password)
- Tester accepts that anonymous live tests from this IP (including the web UI's Live test page) are blocked on node-a for up to one hour afterwards
- `N=http://localhost:3402; BODY='{"patch_id":"pixelplus-087600","mode":"base","max_tokens":4,"messages":[{"role":"user","content":"hi"}]}'`

**Steps**

1. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X POST $N/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"patch_id":"pixelplus-087600","messages":[]}'`
2. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X POST $N/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"patch_id":"no-such-patch","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"hi"}]}'`
3. Run `curl -s -X POST $N/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d "$BODY" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['remaining_quota'], d['quota_limit'], d['patched'], d['base'] is not None)"`
4. Run `for i in $(seq 1 19); do curl -s -X POST $N/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d "$BODY" | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['remaining_quota'])"; done`
5. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X POST $N/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d "$BODY"`
6. Run `curl -s -X POST $N/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -d "$BODY" | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['remaining_quota'], d['quota_limit'])"`
7. Run `curl -s -w ' %{http_code}\n' -X POST http://localhost:3403/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d "$BODY" | tail -c 40`
8. Run `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a logs --kind usage --limit 3`

**Expected**

- Step 1 returns 400 `{"error":"invalid request","issues":[…]}` (messages must have ≥ 1 entry)
- Step 2 returns `{"error":"patch not found"}` with status 500 (a thrown market error, not an HttpError — note: 404 would be the accurate code; report as a finding)
- Step 3 prints `19 20 None True` — the first successful anonymous call leaves 19, base-only mode returns no `patched` answer; steps 1–2 did not consume a try
- Step 4 prints 18, 17, … down to 0
- Step 5 returns `{"error":"quota_chat: free live-test quota exhausted for this hour — buy the patch or run your own node"} 429`
- Step 6 prints `None None` — the operator session is unmetered even from the same IP
- Step 7 returns 200 on node-b (quota is per node process, in memory) with `"quota_limit":20`
- Step 8 shows `usage     [pixelplus-087600] live test pixelplus-087600 (base) by ip:… : base only` entries
- The same quota applies per POST regardless of whether the body uses `patch_id` or `patch_ids` (AZ-101)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/chat (zod min(1) messages, chatQuota check without consume → 429 text, remaining_quota/quota_limit, visitor ip: vs operator:)`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts chatQuota() (limit 20, 1 h window, in-memory Map), chat() 'patch not found' throw → 500 via generic handler`
- `packages/node/src/openapi.ts /api/chat 429 description`

### AZ-067 - Restart the demo cluster with scripts/cluster-restart.sh and confirm data survives, peers re-gossip and the agent buyer still completes a 402 purchase

**Goal:** An operator can bounce all three nodes safely and prove the marketplace (ledger, catalog, verification state, autonomous agent flow) comes back intact.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** cli - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running; `export PATH="$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH"; cd /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace`
- Record before: `curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/info | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['ledger']['records'], d['counts'])"`
- Nobody else is using the demo during the ~1 minute restart

**Steps**

1. Run `scripts/cluster-restart.sh` (without --fresh)
2. Run `tail -f ~/.ngram-cluster/cluster.log` until the line `[cluster] web UI → http://localhost:3402   (B: 3403, C: 3404; homes under /home/<user>/.ngram-cluster; ledger=ain)` appears, then Ctrl+C
3. Run `for p in 3402 3403 3404; do curl -s -o /dev/null -w "$p %{http_code}\n" http://localhost:$p/api/info; done`
4. Re-run the 'before' command and `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a patch ls`
5. Wait ~10 s and run `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a peers ls`
6. Run `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a status`
7. Run `cat ~/.ngram-cluster/nodes.pid | wc -l` and `cat ~/.ngram-cluster/supervisor.pid`
8. Run `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js keys` and fund it: `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a chain fund <agent address> 5`
9. Run `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js catalog`
10. Run `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --patch pixelplus-087600 --pay ain-transfer --json | python3 -c "import sys,json; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(d['success'], d['patch_id'], d['scheme'], d['already_known'], d['restored'], d['steps'][-1])"`

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints one or more `stopped <pid>` lines followed by `cluster starting (log: /home/<user>/.ngram-cluster/cluster.log)`; exit 0
- Step 2: the log shows `[node-a] … [node-b] … [node-c] …` boot lines and the web UI line; a single `retrying in 3 s` line is acceptable (EADDRINUSE while the old port is released — the supervisor only respawns a child that exits within 15 s of start)
- Step 3 prints `3402 200`, `3403 200`, `3404 200` within ~30 s
- Step 4: `records` and `counts` are unchanged (`{'patches': 4, 'listed': 1, 'verifying': 0, 'superseded': 3, 'rejected': 0}` plus anything added by other scenarios); `patch ls` shows the same 4+ rows with identical statuses and ATTEST `2/2` — nothing was re-seeded (`~/.ngram-cluster/node-a/.seeded` marker respected)
- Step 5 shows node-b and node-c rows with a fresh LAST SEEN and FAILURES `0`
- Step 6 shows `runtime     available · Qwen3.8-Flash-Next · hook ok` and `peers       2` (the serving model was not restarted by the script)
- Step 7: nodes.pid has 3 lines; supervisor.pid holds the pid of `node scripts/cluster.mjs`
- Step 8 prints `address     0x…`, `publicKey   …`, `home        /home/<user>/.ngram-agent`; funding prints `✓ funded 0x… with 5 AIN …`
- Step 9 prints `krx-all-2761             LISTED       270053 rows  25 AIN  attest 2/2  KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)` (LISTED only by default)
- Step 10: since pixelplus-087600 is SUPERSEDED and `--patch` does not follow supersede marks, the agent refuses: stderr `agent failed: patch pixelplus-087600 is SUPERSEDED, not LISTED — refusing to buy` and exit 1 — so use `--patch krx-all-2761` (needs ≥ 25 AIN) for the success check: `True krx-all-2761 ain-transfer False True result: SUCCESS — the 402 purchase loop completed` (if the shared model already answers 087600, `already_known` is `True` and `patch_id` is None — still `success` True); node-a's `ledger ls --kind settle --limit 1` shows the agent's address as buyer

**Evidence**

- `scripts/cluster-restart.sh (stop_matching, port wait, log path message)`
- `scripts/cluster.mjs (defs, .seeded marker, nodes.pid/supervisor.pid, respawn only within 15 s, web UI line)`

- `packages/agent/src/agent.ts pickPatch (followLatest false for --patch), 'is SUPERSEDED, not LISTED — refusing to buy', 'result: SUCCESS — the 402 purchase loop completed', already_known`

### AZ-068 - Publish a hidden test listing with `ainize publish --test` and confirm it stays out of public catalogs and counts

**Goal:** A developer can exercise the publish/announce/verify path on the shared chain without polluting what visitors see.

**Priority:** P2 - **Area:** verification - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Same shell setup and node-a login as the draft scenario ($A, $NPZ, $BENCH)
- Tester accepts that one hidden anchor record (and its attestations) is written to the local dev chain (a `chain down` + `scripts/cluster-restart.sh --fresh` resets everything)

**Steps**

1. Run `$A publish $NPZ --id o07-hidden --name "O07 hidden test" --model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next --benchmark "$BENCH" --price 0.1 --test`
2. Run `$A patch ls --status ANNOUNCED,VERIFYING,LISTED` and `curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/catalog | grep -c o07-hidden`
3. Run `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' http://localhost:3402/api/patches/o07-hidden` and the same against :3403
4. Run `$A ledger ls --kind anchor --limit 5`
5. Wait 2 minutes, then run `$B logs --kind verify --limit 30 | grep o07-hidden` on node-b (`B="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-b"`) and `$A patch get o07-hidden | head -1`
6. Run `curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/info | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['counts'])"` and `curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/chat/patches | grep -c o07-hidden`

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `✓ draft created: o07-hidden  (2,992 rows, …)` and then `✓ announced o07-hidden → ledger record <hash> (verifiers will now attest; quorum lists it)`
- Step 2: `patch ls` output does not contain o07-hidden; grep count is 0 (test-visibility anchors are filtered from /api/catalog on every node unless `includeTestAnchors` is set)
- Step 3 returns 200 on both nodes — direct lookup works by design (hidden from listings, not from direct access)
- Step 4 shows the raw ledger record `anchor … o07-hidden · Qwen3.8-Flash-Next · 2992 rows` — the record exists on the shared chain even though catalogs hide it
- Step 5: verifiers DO attest hidden anchors: node-b logs `attested o07-hidden: PASS (vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next)` and `patch get` shows `LISTED` — while `patch ls` / `/api/catalog` still omit it
- Step 6 still shows `{'patches': 4, 'listed': 1, 'verifying': 0, 'superseded': 3, 'rejected': 0}` and the Live test list count is 0 — hidden anchors never reach visitors

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/patch.ts patchPublish (visibility: 'test')`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts catalog() filter on anchor.visibility !== 'test' || cfg.includeTestAnchors; entry() uses catalogAll()`
- `packages/core/src/types.ts includeTestAnchors`
- `packages/node/src/verifier.ts round() iterates catalogAll() (verifies test-visibility anchors too)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/ledger (unfiltered), GET /api/info counts, GET /api/chat/patches`

### AZ-069 - Show that a verifier whose serving API is down keeps retrying for 15 minutes instead of attesting hash-only

**Goal:** A node operator can trust that a serving restart on a verifier delays the attestation rather than producing a degraded 'hash-only' vote on a runtime-capable node.

**Priority:** P2 - **Area:** verification - **Automation:** manual

**Preconditions**

- node-d from the fourth-node scenario stopped; then re-pointed to a dead serving API: `$D config set runtime.api http://localhost:8999` (repo stays /mnt/newdata/qwen3.8) and `$D config set roles verifier`; `$D start -d`
- A patch is currently ANNOUNCED or VERIFYING on the network — run the announce scenario's publish step (or publish another public patch with a unique schema) right after node-d is up
- Demo cluster running

**Steps**

1. Run `$D status`
2. Run `$D logs --limit 20` within the first minute after the announce
3. Wait 60 s and run `$D logs --kind verifier --limit 5` again
4. Run `$D config set runtime.api http://localhost:8000`, then `$D stop && $D start -d`
5. Wait ~60 s (the shared lock may be held by node-b/node-c) and run `$D logs --kind verify --limit 5`
6. Run `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a patch get <announced id>`

**Expected**

- Step 1 shows `runtime     unavailable (serving API unreachable)`
- Step 2 shows an info `verifier` line `verifying <id> (<name>)`, a `blob` line for the body fetch, then a `warn verifier` line `verify <id> failed: runtime unavailable (serving API unreachable) — waiting up to 15 min before hash-only fallback` (repo present + real model + benchmark samples ⇒ the node is expected to have a runtime, so it waits)
- Step 3 shows the same warning repeated every ~5 s; the minute count is rounded (`waiting up to 15 min` for the first ~30 s, `14 min` after ~1 minute); no `attest` record from node-d exists (`patch records <id>` has no author = node-d)
- Step 4 prints `✓ runtime.api = "http://localhost:8000"  (the node reads config.json when it starts)` on stdout and, because node-d is still running at that moment, the warning `! the node in $H is running (pid N) and keeps using the value it started with — restart it to apply this (`ainize stop` then `ainize start -d`)` on stderr; then `✓ stopped node` / `✓ node started in the background`
- Step 5 shows `attested <id>: PASS (vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next)` — the pending patch was verified for real once the runtime came back, never as hash-only
- Step 6 shows node-d's attestation row with `VERIFIED ON vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next` (third attestation; status LISTED) and the verification line `2/2 passed ✓ quorum (+1 more independent attestation)` — the numerator is clamped to the quorum, so a third verifier can never make it read `3/2` (critique 2 item 146). If node-d had instead written `hash-only` with `benchmark: not executed …` within the first 15 minutes, the grace period is broken — report it

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/verifier.ts RUNTIME_GRACE_MS = 15 min, graceLeft(), 'waiting up to N min before hash-only fallback' message (Math.round)`
- `packages/node/src/runtime.ts status() error 'serving API unreachable'`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/init.ts configSet message`

### AZ-070 - Check the aindrive mirror: `drive status --files`, `drive sync`, `drive up` before pairing, and the changes API guard

**Goal:** An operator can see which node files are mirrored for sharing, refresh the mirror, and gets a clear pairing hint instead of a silent failure.

**Priority:** P2 - **Area:** drive - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running; node-a operator logged in (`A=…node-a`); node-a drive not paired (`curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/drive` shows `"configured":false`)

**Steps**

1. Run `$A drive status`
2. Run `$A drive status --files | head -12`
3. Run `$A drive sync`
4. Run `$A drive up`
5. Run `$A drive stop`
6. Run `curl -s 'http://localhost:3402/api/drive/changes?path=ledger/records.jsonl' | head -c 300` and `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' 'http://localhost:3402/api/drive/changes?path=../config.json'`
7. Run `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -X POST http://localhost:3402/api/drive -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"action":"up"}'` (no auth)
8. Run `$A drive login --no-open` and press Ctrl+C once the link is printed

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `folder    /home/<user>/.ngram-cluster/node-a/data/drive`, `paired    no — run \`ainize drive login\``, `agent     stopped`, `server    https://aindrive.ainetwork.ai`, `drive id  -`, `url       -`, `files     <n>`
- Step 2 adds a `PATH SIZE MODIFIED` table containing `branches/finance__KRX-latest.json`, `branches/finance__KRX-history.json`, `ledger/records.jsonl` and patch folders
- Step 3 prints `✓ drive folder synced (<n> file(s) written)`
- Step 4 prints `! drive not paired yet — run: cd /home/<user>/.ngram-cluster/node-a/data/drive && npx aindrive login --server https://aindrive.ainetwork.ai   # one-time browser pairing, then: ainize drive up`
- Step 5 prints `✓ not running`
- Step 6: the first call returns JSON for the mirrored ledger file; the path-traversal call returns 400 (`{"error":"path must be relative to the drive folder"}`)
- Step 7 returns 401
- Step 8 prints the `aindrive pairing` header with `folder :` and `server :` lines and the dim instructions `A browser sign-in link will be printed — open it, click Authorize, and this folder becomes a drive.` / `After pairing, Ctrl+C here and run \`ainize drive up\` to serve it in the background.` (or `error: aindrive CLI not installed (npm install aindrive)` if the package is missing)

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/drive.ts driveStatus/driveAction/driveLogin strings`
- `packages/node/src/drive.ts up()/stop() messages, login_hint`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts /api/drive, /api/drive/changes path guard`

### AZ-204 - The chat body and an uploaded file produce byte-identical artifacts: POST /api/teach/jobs {facts} freezes one canonical dataset, and re-sending it makes no second copy

**Goal:** Verify at the API the claim the UI makes: the legacy chat body materialises a dataset server-side whose bytes, name, sha256 and download are exactly what the file door would have produced, and that the same corrections never create a second dataset.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** api - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- node-u in STUB mode (teach.stubOffline true, backend stub) — no model server involved
- A fresh teaching key; every request signed with the request-bound v2 x-ngram-auth (teach:<nodeAddress>:<METHOD>:<path+query>:<ts>[:<sha256 body>])

**Steps**

1. POST /api/teach/jobs with body {"patch_ids":[],"builds_on_context":false,"facts":[{"prompt":"Who operates the Ainize teaching node AZ204?","answer":"Comcom","alt_prompt":"Which company runs the Ainize teaching node AZ204?"},{"prompt":"What year did the AZ204 pilot start?","answer":"2020"}]}
2. GET /api/teach/datasets (same key) and GET /api/teach/datasets/<dsId>
3. GET /api/teach/datasets/<dsId>/download
4. POST /api/teach/jobs again with the identical facts array
5. GET /api/teach/datasets again

**Expected**

- First POST → 202 with {job, quota}; job.dataset.source === "chat", job.dataset.rows === 2, job.dataset.trained_rows === 2; quota carries key_remaining, ip_remaining, rows_remaining, rows_ip_remaining
- The dataset is named your-dataset-<YYYY-MM-DD> (UTC day), status 'in_use' while the lesson runs, revision 1, source 'chat', size_bytes = the canonical bytes length
- dataset.sha256 === sha256 of exactly: {"prompt":"Who operates the Ainize teaching node AZ204?","answer":"Comcom","alt_prompt":"Which company runs the Ainize teaching node AZ204?"}\n{"prompt":"What year did the AZ204 pilot start?","answer":"2020"}\n
- Download → 200, content-type application/x-ndjson, header x-content-sha256 === dataset.sha256, content-disposition attachment; filename="dataset-<dsId>-r1.jsonl", and the body bytes are byte-identical to the literal above
- Second POST → 202 with a NEW job id but the SAME dataset id (findTeachDatasetBySha); GET /api/teach/datasets still returns exactly one dataset for this key, still revision 1
- Cleanup: both jobs and the dataset deleted (200)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts:572-589 (POST /api/teach/jobs, dataset_id XOR facts)`
- `packages/node/src/teach.ts:655-663; packages/node/src/teach-datasets.ts:179-200 (canonicalBytes, findTeachDatasetBySha idempotence), :438 defaultName, :264 download`
- `endpoint POST /api/teach/jobs · GET /api/teach/datasets/{id}/download`

- `--json` output is a single JSON object with items[].{id,name,status:"staged",source:"upload",sha256,revision:1,rows:3,invalid_rows:2,summary:{source_rows:5,accepted:3,duplicates:1,empty:1,…},expires_at} and prints no human table

### AZ-209 - CLI failure contract: a refused upload still prints the per-line report, and the exit codes are 0 / 1 / 2

**Goal:** A terminal user must learn WHICH lines the node could not read even when the upload is refused, and scripts must be able to tell "your input was wrong" (1) from "the node is not there" (2).

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** cli - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- node-u on http://localhost:3422; a throwaway CLI home /tmp/az209
- Fixture ./az-bad.txt, exactly:
this file has no question and answer at all
just prose, nothing else

**Steps**

1. node … --home /tmp/az209 teach dataset ./az-bad.txt ; echo $?
2. node … --home /tmp/az209 teach train /no/such/file.csv ; echo $?
3. node … --home /tmp/az209 teach dataset get 00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000 ; echo $?
4. node … --home /tmp/az209 teach train <a real dataset id> --effort ultra ; echo $?
5. node --node http://localhost:39999 --home /tmp/az209 teach dataset ls ; echo $?

**Expected**

- The refused upload prints on stderr, BEFORE the error line: "0 of 2 lines will train · not used: 2 empty", the heading "what the node read" and the full table (line 1 empty "this question has no answer", line 2 empty "this question has no answer"), then "error: dataset_empty: that file has no usable questions — every line needs a question and a right answer"; exit code 1
- Missing file: "error: not a dataset id or a file: /no/such/file.csv — `ainize teach dataset ls` lists your datasets"; exit code 1
- Unknown id: "error: dataset_not_found: no such dataset on this node"; exit code 1
- Invalid --effort: yargs prints "Invalid values:\n  Argument: effort, Given: \"ultra\", Choices: \"quick\", \"balanced\", \"thorough\""; exit code 1; no request is sent to the node
- Unreachable node: "error: cannot reach node at http://localhost:39999 (fetch failed). Is it running? Try `ainize start` or pass --node <url>."; exit code 2 — the only non-1 failure code
- Cleanup: remove /tmp/az209

**Evidence**

- `packages/cli/src/commands/teach-dataset.ts:203-212 (withReport prints the report of a refused upload), :300-306 (teachTrain path check), :270-290 (trainingSpec validation)`
- `packages/cli/src/context.ts:36 (CliError exitCode default 1); packages/cli/src/client.ts:28 (exit 2 on unreachable); packages/cli/src/bin.ts:44`

### AZ-211 - `teach dataset get <id> -o questions.jsonl` round-trips: the saved bytes verify against the fingerprint and re-uploading them lands on the same dataset

**Goal:** Reproducibility of a lesson from its own questions: the CLI must hand back the canonical bytes, say whether they matched the fingerprint, and re-uploading that file must not create a copy.

5. node … --home /tmp/az211 teach dataset get <dsId> -o ./questions.csv --format csv

**Expected**

- -o prints "✓ saved <abs path>/questions.jsonl (284 B) · fingerprint verified — re-uploading it lands on this same dataset"; exit 0

{"prompt":"Who operates the Ainize teaching node AZ208?","answer":"Comcom","alt_prompt":"Which company runs the Ainize teaching node AZ208?"}
{"prompt":"What year did the AZ208 pilot start?","answer":"2020"}
{"prompt":"What does an AZ208 lesson produce?","answer":"A knowledge file"}
- sha256(questions.jsonl) === dataset.sha256 exactly
- Re-uploading questions.jsonl returns the SAME dataset id and the same fingerprint, and prints "· questions.jsonl is already on this node — same questions, same dataset, no second copy"
- --all prints the heading "every line" and all 5 source lines with their status (3 × ok, 1 duplicate, 1 empty); without --all only the 2 problem lines are shown
- --format csv writes a csv rendering; the CLI verifies it against the response header x-content-sha256 (not the dataset fingerprint) and does NOT claim "fingerprint verified"
- Cleanup: dataset removed with `teach dataset rm <id>` → "✓ dataset <id> deleted. The lessons trained from it are kept — but they can no longer be re-trained from their questions."

### AZ-212 - `teach jobs` / `teach status` from the terminal: my lessons with their dataset, the node's teaching policy, and a foreign key sees status only

**Goal:** The terminal must answer the three questions the browser answers — what have I taught, what will this node accept, and what is one lesson doing — with the dataset attached and without leaking a lesson body to a key that does not own it.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** cli - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- node-u on http://localhost:3422; CLI home /tmp/az212 holding key K1 with one READY lesson from a 3-question dataset; a second CLI home /tmp/az212b holding a different key K2

**Steps**

1. node … --home /tmp/az212 teach jobs
2. node … --home /tmp/az212 teach jobs --dataset <dsId>
3. node … --home /tmp/az212 teach status http://localhost:3422
4. node … --home /tmp/az212b teach status <jobId>
5. node … --home /tmp/az212 teach dataset rm <dsId> then re-run teach jobs

**Expected**

- `teach jobs` prints "your lessons on http://localhost:3422" and a table LESSON / NAME / STATUS / DATASET / QUESTIONS / EFFORT / PUBLISHED AS / UPDATED; the row shows the lesson id, STATUS READY, DATASET <first 8 of dsId>, QUESTIONS "3 / 3", EFFORT quick|balanced|thorough, PUBLISHED AS the draft id (dimmed) — plus the footer "one lesson: ainize teach status <lesson-id>   ·   its questions: ainize teach dataset get <dataset-id>"
- --dataset <dsId> keeps only lessons whose dataset.id matches; --dataset <other uuid> prints the empty-table message "no lessons yet"
- `teach status <node-url>` prints the policy block: "Teaching on teachable-u  accepting lessons  http://localhost:3422", trainer "ready · backend stub (no GPU training on this node)", publish "auto — published lessons are announced at once", queue "0 / 10 lessons · 0 / 2000 questions waiting", limits "200 questions per lesson (default) · 3 lessons per key and 5 per IP a day · prompt ≤ 400 / answer ≤ 200 chars", and a datasets line "up to 2,000 questions per file · files ≤ 3.8 MB · jsonl json csv tsv txt · 10 uploads and 300 trained questions per key a day · kept 7 days", effort presets, data-provider share 70 %, then the two doors ("teach from a file:" / "teach in chat: http://localhost:3422/chat?teach=1")
- With key K2 the same lesson prints only "Lesson <id>  READY" plus "status only — pass your teaching key (--key-file <backup.json>) to see the lesson body" — no facts, no draft id, no dataset; exit 0
- After `teach dataset rm <dsId>` the lesson is still listed by `teach jobs` with DATASET "<8 hex> (deleted)" and QUESTIONS unchanged; `teach dataset ls` still shows the row with STATE deleted
- Cleanup: the lesson deleted; /tmp/az212 and /tmp/az212b removed

### AZ-213 - OpenAPI documents every dataset route the node actually serves — path, method, auth and error codes

**Goal:** A developer reading GET /api/openapi.json must be able to drive the whole dataset pipeline; a route that exists but is undocumented (or documented but gone) is a defect.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** api - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- node-u on http://localhost:3422
- The repo checkout is available to the test (packages/node/src/api.ts is read as the source of truth for what is registered)

**Steps**

1. Extract from packages/node/src/api.ts every `router.<method>('/api/teach/datasets…')`, `'/api/teach/samples…'` and `'/api/me/teach/datasets'` registration and normalise :id → {id}, :kind → {kind}
2. GET http://localhost:3422/api/openapi.json and collect the same paths with their methods
3. Compare the two sets exactly, both directions
4. For each documented dataset path, read its tags, parameters/security and documented response codes
5. Probe each route live once (unsigned) and check the status is one the document lists

**Expected**

- The two sets are equal, and are exactly: POST+GET /api/teach/datasets; GET+PATCH+DELETE /api/teach/datasets/{id}; GET /api/teach/datasets/{id}/rows; POST /api/teach/datasets/{id}/reparse; POST /api/teach/datasets/{id}/fork; GET /api/teach/datasets/{id}/download; GET /api/teach/samples; GET /api/teach/samples/{kind}; GET /api/me/teach/datasets
- Every /api/teach/dataset* operation is tagged "Teach" and declares the x-ngram-auth header parameter; /api/me/teach/datasets is tagged "Operator" and declares the operator bearer security scheme
- The document states the owner-only rule in prose on GET /api/teach/datasets/{id} ("Owner (signed) or operator. Anyone else gets 404 — a stranger is never told that a dataset exists.") and documents the named failures: 400 dataset_empty / dataset_format / dataset_hash, 404 dataset_not_found, 409 dataset_in_use, 413 dataset_too_large, 429 quota_dataset / quota_bytes / rate_limited
- POST /api/teach/datasets describes both request shapes (multipart file door and the JSON chat/inline/sample body) in one operation
- An unsigned GET /api/teach/datasets/{any id} returns 404 (a documented code), never 401/500; GET /api/teach/samples returns 200 with cache-control public, max-age=3600
- openapi version is 3.1.0 and GET /api/docs returns exactly the keys ['cli','node','openapi']

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/openapi.ts:200-262 (Teach dataset paths), :259 (/api/me/teach/datasets)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts:464-538, :693-700 (the registrations)`
- `endpoint GET /api/openapi.json · GET /api/docs`

### AZ-214 - Quotas are counted in QUESTIONS, not lessons: rows_per_key_per_day and rows_per_ip_per_day refuse the lesson with the numbers in the message

**Goal:** GPU time is spent per question, so the node's daily budget must be enforced on rows, charged when the lesson is created, reported back on every job creation, and refused with a message a person can act on. complements the v1 AZ-115 (lessons per day) — this is the per-QUESTION budget.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** api - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- node-u on http://localhost:3422, STUB mode
- Operator bearer for node-u (password teachable-pass)
- A fresh teaching key with no rows spent today
- Record the current teach policy before changing it and restore it afterwards

**Steps**

1. PATCH /api/me/teach/policy {"rows_per_key_per_day": 3} (operator bearer) and read back GET /api/teach/policy
2. Create a 2-question dataset with that key and POST /api/teach/jobs {dataset_id, patch_ids:[]}
3. POST /api/teach/jobs again for the same dataset (2 more questions)
4. PATCH /api/me/teach/policy {"rows_per_key_per_day": null, "rows_per_ip_per_day": 2} and repeat with a second fresh key from the same IP
5. Restore the recorded policy and delete everything created

**Expected**

- GET /api/teach/policy reflects the change immediately: limits.rows_per_key_per_day === 3 (the visitor-facing policy is what the operator set; policy cache invalidated)
- First POST → 202 and quota === { key_remaining, ip_remaining, rows_remaining: 1, rows_ip_remaining: … } — 2 of 3 questions charged at creation time
- Second POST → 429 with body {"error":"quota_rows: you have 1 of 3 questions left to teach on this node today", rows_remaining:1, rows_ip_remaining:<n>, limit:3, asked:2} — no job row is created (GET /api/teach/jobs count unchanged) and no rows are charged
- With rows_per_ip_per_day 2 exhausted, a DIFFERENT key from the same IP is refused too: 429 "quota_rows: this address has <n> of 2 questions left to teach on this node today" — the per-IP budget is independent of the key
- The browser maps that error to the visitor sentence "You have used up the questions you can teach on this node today. Come back tomorrow, or run your own node — the instructions come with every lesson you download." (teach.err.quota_rows)
- After restore, GET /api/teach/policy limits equal the recorded values (rows_per_key_per_day 300, rows_per_ip_per_day 500 on the dev node)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/teach.ts:717-721 (quota_rows for key and IP, with details), :741 chargeRows`
- `packages/node/src/teach-datasets.ts:118-133 (quota), :151-156 (chargeRows)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts:663-690 (PATCH /api/me/teach/policy rows_per_key_per_day / rows_per_ip_per_day)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/teach.ts:575 (teach.err.quota_rows)`

### AZ-215 - Owner-only reads: a stranger's key, an unsigned request and a replayed signature all get 404 — and the operator can read but cannot edit someone's dataset

**Goal:** A dataset is private to the teaching key that created it. A stranger must not even learn that it exists, and the operator's moderation power must be read/delete, never a silent edit under the owner's name.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** api - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- node-u on http://localhost:3422
- Key K1 owns dataset D (2 questions) with one lesson; key K2 is a different, unbanned teaching key; operator bearer available

**Steps**

1. With K2, signed v2: GET /api/teach/datasets/D, GET /api/teach/datasets/D/rows, GET /api/teach/datasets/D/download, PATCH /api/teach/datasets/D {name:'stolen'}, DELETE /api/teach/datasets/D
2. Unsigned (no x-ngram-auth): GET /api/teach/datasets/D and GET /api/teach/datasets
3. Replay a valid K1 header for GET /api/teach/datasets/D a second time; and send a K1 header with ts = now - 10 min
4. With the operator bearer only: GET /api/teach/datasets/D, then PATCH /api/teach/datasets/D {name:'operator rename'}
5. With K1: GET /api/teach/datasets/D

**Expected**

- Every K2 request returns 404 {"error":"dataset_not_found: no such dataset on this node"} — the same body for read, rows, download, patch and delete, so existence is never disclosed; nothing about D changes
- Unsigned GET of D → 404 dataset_not_found; unsigned GET /api/teach/datasets → 401 invalid_signature (the list route requires a key, the item route hides behind 404)
- A replayed v2 header → 401 invalid_signature (single-use); a header 10 minutes old → 401 (±5 min skew)
- Operator bearer GET /api/teach/datasets/D → 200 with the full dataset (moderation read)
- Operator bearer PATCH /api/teach/datasets/D → 401 invalid_signature: the patch route requires the owner's teaching key; the dataset's name and revision are unchanged
- K1 GET /api/teach/datasets/D → 200, name and revision exactly as before the whole sequence

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts:497-530 (owned(id, teacherOf(req), isOperator(req)); PATCH/fork/reparse use requireTeacher only)`
- `packages/node/src/teach-auth.ts:40-80 (v2 request binding, single-use replay cache, TEACH_AUTH_SKEW_MS)`
- `packages/node/src/openapi.ts:210 ("Anyone else gets 404 — a stranger is never told that a dataset exists.")`

### AZ-216 - The rows report is the contract behind the preview table: /rows paging, status filter and summary must agree with the dataset and with the download

**Goal:** The wizard, the CLI and any script read the same report; its shape, its counts and its relationship to the canonical bytes must be exact, or "it looked fine in the preview" bugs return.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** api - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- node-u on http://localhost:3422
- Upload (multipart, x-ngram-dataset-sha256 set) the AZ-208 fixture az-cli.csv with key K1 — 5 source lines, 3 accepted, 1 duplicate, 1 empty

**Steps**

1. GET /api/teach/datasets/<id>/rows (defaults)
2. GET /api/teach/datasets/<id>/rows?status=ok and ?status=rejected
3. GET /api/teach/datasets/<id>/rows?offset=1&limit=2
4. GET /api/teach/datasets/<id>/download and ?format=csv

**Expected**

- The default page is {total, source_rows: 5, offset: 0, limit: 50, summary, items[]}; summary === {source_rows:5, accepted:3, fixed:0, rejected:2, duplicates:1, conflicts:0, blocked:0, too_long:0, empty:1, not_parsed:0, over_cap:0, shared_ending:0, langs:{…}}
- Each item carries index (null for a rejected line), line (the SOURCE line number: 2..6 for a header csv), status, prompt/answer/alt_prompt or raw, and detail — line 5 status 'duplicate' detail "the same question and answer as line 3", line 6 status 'empty' detail "this question has no answer"
- ?status=ok returns exactly the 3 trainable rows with contiguous index 0,1,2; ?status=rejected returns exactly the 2 problem rows; total reflects the filter
- offset=1&limit=2 returns 2 items starting at the second row, with offset:1 and limit:2 echoed back
- download (jsonl) → x-content-sha256 === dataset.sha256 === sha256(body); download?format=csv → content-type text/csv, a different x-content-sha256, and its body is NOT the fingerprint subject
- Cleanup: dataset deleted

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts:501-507 (rows page shape), :470-495 (multipart create, declaredSha256)`
- `packages/node/src/teach-datasets.ts:160-170 (dataset_hash), :264-276 (download jsonl/csv sha)`
