### AZ-227 - Challenge a listed knowledge and confirm it stops selling until it is re-verified

**Goal:** A verifier that disputes a result can take the knowledge off sale, everyone can read why, and a re-verification puts it back — instead of the item selling at full price with a red chip.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- A PRIVATE three-node cluster on the local ledger (NGRAM_CLUSTER_HOME=<tmp> NGRAM_PORT_BASE=3512 NGRAM_LEDGER=local NGRAM_SEED=0 scripts/cluster-restart.sh). Never the shared demo chain: a challenge record is permanent and would take a demo knowledge off sale for everyone
- One knowledge published by node-a and LISTED (2/2) — GET /x402/patch/<id> answers 402
- Operator sessions on node-b and node-c

**Steps**

1. GET /x402/patch/<id> on the seller and note the 402
2. On node-c: `ainize patch challenge <id> --reason "the ticker codes are wrong"`
3. GET /x402/patch/<id> again
4. On node-b: `ainize patch buy <id>`
5. Open the knowledge page and the seller's manage page in the browser
6. Wait for the verifiers' next automatic round (verifier.intervalMs) and repeat the GET

**Expected**

- Before the challenge the gateway answers 402 with the payment requirements
- GET /api/patches/<id> reports status CHALLENGED, quorum_ok true (the quorum is still met) and sellable false, with open_challenge {challenger, reason, created_at}
- The gateway answers 423 'a verifier has challenged this knowledge — re-verification pending, so it is not for sale (0x…: "the ticker codes are wrong")' — not a discounted 402
- `ainize patch buy` refuses with the same sentence and no settlement record is written
- The knowledge page shows a warning banner naming the challenger, the reason, when, and what happens next; the Buy tab says 'Challenged knowledge is not on sale at any price — the sale is stopped, not discounted.'; the seller's manage page shows the same banner
- `ainize patch get <id>` prints a 'challenges' block with the challenger, reason and which challenge is open
- After a verifier re-runs the benchmark (the automatic round now re-verifies a knowledge it has already attested when a challenge is open), the newer attestation replaces its earlier one, the status returns to LISTED, sellable is true again and the gateway answers 402

**Evidence**

- `packages/core/src/catalog.ts deriveCatalog (sellable, open_challenge, effectiveAttestation)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id (423 + challengedMessage), packages/node/src/market.ts buy()`
- `packages/node/src/verifier.ts round() (re-verify a CHALLENGED anchor it already attested)`
- `packages/web/src/pages/PatchPage.tsx challenge banner + Buy tab, packages/web/src/pages/ManagePage.tsx challenged banner`
- `packages/node/test/trust.test.ts item 153 (the same flow on three nodes, automated)`

## AI agent / automation

### AZ-071 - Run the autonomous buyer end to end: detect the gap, pay 25 AIN via 402, verify the hash, load and restore

**Goal:** An AI agent closes its own knowledge gap without a human: it notices a wrong answer, buys the verified knowledge with automatic payment, checks integrity and loads it into the running model.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** agent - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running on the AIN ledger; vLLM at http://localhost:8000 with the live-apply hook; krx-all-2761 LISTED (2/2) and NOT currently loaded in the shared model (GET http://localhost:3402/api/runtime applied = [])
- Node 24 on PATH: `export PATH="$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH"; cd /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace`
- Agent identity funded with ≥ 50 AIN: `ADDR=$(node packages/agent/dist/bin.js keys --json | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['address'])")`; `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js chain fund $ADDR 60`
- Record before: settle count `curl -s 'http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1000' | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(len(json.load(sys.stdin)['records']))"`, downloads from GET /api/catalog for krx-all-2761, and node-a's balance from GET http://localhost:3402/api/chain
- node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. Run `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --patch krx-all-2761` (defaults: question '픽셀플러스 종목코드 알려줘', prompt '종목코드 픽셀플러스 ', expect 087600, pay auto) and read the log; `echo $?`
2. Re-read the settle count, krx-all-2761 downloads and node-a's balance
3. Run the same command again with `--json` and read the JSON
4. Run `ls -l ~/.ngram-agent/patches/` and `curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/runtime | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['applied'])"`

**Expected**

- Step 1 log, in order: `[0] agent 0x…  market http://localhost:3402`; `[1] question: "픽셀플러스 종목코드 알려줘"`; `    current answer: "…"  → wrong/unknown — knowledge purchase needed`; `[2] searching the catalog (ledger anchors + verification quorum)`; `    candidate: krx-all-2761  347.8 MB  270053 rows  price 25 AIN  quorum met by 2 verifier(s) (vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, vllm:Qwen3.8-Flash-Next)` (size uses 1e6 bytes per MB, hence 347.8 MB vs the UI's 331.7 MB); `[3] requesting the resource → GET http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`; `    402 Payment Required: 25 AIN → 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5  (ain-transfer, nonce <24 hex>)`; `[4] paying (AIN transfer on chain) and retrying with the proof`; `    settled: tx 0x…  {"settled":true,"tx":"0x…","royalty":{"0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5":"25"}}`; `[5] manifest sha256 <16 hex>… matches · body sha256 57c9346349afd6fa… (same as the on-ledger anchor)`; `    received 347.8 MB from http://localhost:3402/p2p/blob/57c9346349afd6fa…`; `    sha256 == on-ledger anchor hash — integrity holds no matter which peer served it`; `[6] loading into the running model (no restart)`; `    <scripts/patch.py apply output>`; `    answer with knowledge: "087600…"  → correct`; `    restored (subscription ended): <patch.py remove output>`; `result: SUCCESS — the 402 purchase loop completed`; exit code 0
- Step 2: settle count +1 (buyer = agent address, amount '25', scheme 'ain-transfer'), krx-all-2761 downloads +1, node-a balance +25 AIN, agent balance −25 AIN
- Step 3: the second run pays again (25 AIN) but prints `    body already present — download skipped` in step [5]; the JSON has identity=<agent address>, before (wrong answer), after starting with 087600, already_known=false, patch_id='krx-all-2761', scheme='ain-transfer', tx_hash '0x…', amount='25', sha256='57c9346349afd6fa1b0475340b9b041ee0027753c05463d4bb204a5a4726c642', path '/home/<user>/.ngram-agent/patches/57c9…npz', applied=true, restored=true, success=true and the steps array; nothing else on stdout; exit 0
- Step 4: the cached body 57c9346349afd6fa…6c642.npz (347,829,014 bytes) is in the agent home; the node's applied list is [] (the agent applies via scripts/patch.py directly and restores the shared table afterwards)

**Evidence**

- `packages/agent/src/agent.ts runAgent() step strings ([0]–[6], 'wrong/unknown — knowledge purchase needed', 'candidate:', 'settled: tx', 'received … from', 'body already present — download skipped', 'answer with knowledge:', 'restored (subscription ended):', 'result: SUCCESS — the 402 purchase loop completed')`
- `packages/agent/src/bin.ts run defaults (question/prompt/expect/pay/repo), --json output, exit codes`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id (402 → X-PAYMENT → 200 manifest + x-payment-response), GET /p2p/blob/:sha`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts settlePayment() (settle record, royalty to the single author), issueManifest()`
- `packages/core/src/catalog.ts royaltySplit()`

### AZ-072 - Verify the x402 402 challenge contract on the seller gateway (header, body, CORS exposure, non-seller and unknown ids)

**Goal:** Any HTTP client (ain-js compatible) can discover price, payee and nonce for a knowledge patch from the 402 response alone.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- Seller node http://localhost:3402 up; krx-all-2761 LISTED (author = node-a; payTo = GET /api/info node.address, currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)
- curl and python3 available

**Steps**

1. `curl -s -D - -o /tmp/x402.json http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761` and read status line and headers
2. Decode the header: `curl -s -D - -o /dev/null http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761 | grep -i '^x-payment-required:' | cut -d' ' -f2 | base64 -d`
3. Compare with body: `python3 -c "import json;d=json.load(open('/tmp/x402.json'));print(d['x402Version'],d['error'],d['requirements']==d['accepts'],d['requirements'][0])"`
4. Repeat step 1 twice more and compare the `nonce` and `expires_at` values
5. `curl -s -i http://localhost:3403/x402/patch/krx-all-2761` (verifier node, not the seller)
6. `curl -s -i http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/does-not-exist`

**Expected**

- Status 'HTTP/1.1 402 Payment Required'; headers include 'www-authenticate: x402', 'x-payment-required: <base64>', 'access-control-expose-headers: x-payment-required, x-payment-tx-hash, x-payment-currency, x-payment-response, x-content-sha256' and 'access-control-allow-headers: content-type, authorization, x-payment, x-ngram-auth, x-ngram-buyer'
- Decoded header is a JSON array with exactly one requirement: {"scheme":"ain-transfer","network":"ain:local","asset":"AIN","payTo":"0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5","maxAmountRequired":"25","resource":"/x402/patch/krx-all-2761","description":"Knowledge patch krx-all-2761 (270053 rows, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next)","nonce":"<24 hex>","expires_at":<ms epoch>}
- Body prints '1 payment required True' and body.requirements[0] equals the decoded header entry; expires_at is about 10 minutes (600000 ms) after the request
- Each 402 issues a fresh nonce (three different 24-hex nonces)
- Non-seller node answers 409 {"error":"not sold here; gateway is http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761"}
- Unknown id answers 404 {"error":"patch not found"}

**Evidence**

- `packages/core/src/x402.ts (X402_HEADER_REQUIRED='x-payment-required', encodeRequirements base64, newNonce 12 bytes)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id (402 branch, 409 'not sold here', 404 'patch not found'); packages/node/src/server.ts CORS expose/allow headers`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts requirementsFor() (10-minute nonce TTL, asset 'AIN' when ledger.kind==='ain')`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/docs.ts 'docs.x402.steps'`

### AZ-073 - Verify the settled 200 response contract and its ledger/event side effects after an ain-transfer payment

**Goal:** After paying, an agent receives a tamper-evident manifest plus receipts (tx hash, royalty split) that match what the seller recorded on the ledger.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- The end-to-end agent run completed once (a fresh settle record exists for krx-all-2761 with buyer = agent address)
- Agent address known from `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js keys`; node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. From the agent log copy the '    settled: tx 0x…' hash and the echoed x-payment-response JSON
2. GET http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1 and read records[0].body
3. GET http://localhost:3402/api/events?kind=trade&limit=1
4. GET http://localhost:3402/api/patches/krx-all-2761/records and count kind='settle' records whose body.buyer is the agent address
5. Check on-chain: `curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8081/json-rpc -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"ain_get","params":{"protoVer":"1.0.0","type":"GET_VALUE","ref":"/apps/knowledge/market/settlements/krx-all-2761"}}'`
6. Check the blob gate without credentials: `curl -s -i http://localhost:3402/p2p/blob/57c9346349afd6fa1b0475340b9b041ee0027753c05463d4bb204a5a4726c642 | head -5`

**Expected**

- x-payment-response JSON has settled=true, tx equal to the logged tx hash, and royalty {"0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5":"25"} (single-author lineage → whole price to seller)
- Newest settle body: patch_id='krx-all-2761', seller=<node-a address>, buyer=<agent address>, amount='25', currency='AIN', scheme='ain-transfer', tx_hash=<same tx>, billing='per_download', royalty as above
- Newest trade event message: 'sold krx-all-2761 to 0x<first 10 of agent>… for 25 AIN (ain-transfer)' with data.tx = the tx hash; GET /api/events?kind=royalty returns no new event (no royalty leaves the seller for same-author lineage)
- /api/patches/krx-all-2761/records contains the settle record for the agent
- On-chain /apps/knowledge/market/settlements/krx-all-2761 has a child keyed by keyOf(tx_hash) whose body carries the same fields (seller, buyer, amount '25', scheme 'ain-transfer') — compare the body, not the raw hash key
- Unauthenticated blob GET answers 402 {"error":"payment required: buy the patch via /x402/patch/:id (verifiers and authors are exempt)"} — the body is only served with the manifest's download_token or a signed x-ngram-auth header from a settled buyer (which the agent used in its step [5])

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id success branch (x-payment-tx-hash, x-payment-currency, x-payment-response, x-content-sha256), GET /p2p/blob/:sha (402 message), GET /api/ledger, /api/events, /api/patches/:id/records`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts settlePayment() (settle record, 'trade' event, royalty transfer skipped when addr === this.address), mayDownload()`
- `packages/core/src/catalog.ts royaltySplit()`
- `packages/core/src/ain-ledger.ts settle → /apps/knowledge/market/settlements/$id/$keyOf(tx)`

### AZ-074 - Refuse to pay when the agent's AIN balance is below the price, then succeed after funding

**Goal:** An unfunded agent fails fast with an actionable message and never creates a half-completed purchase.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** agent - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Cluster and chain up; Node 24 on PATH; krx-all-2761 LISTED and not loaded in the shared model
- A fresh, empty agent home directory path chosen, e.g. /tmp/claude-agent-poor (must not exist yet)

**Steps**

1. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js keys --home /tmp/claude-agent-poor` (creates a new identity with 0 AIN); note the address
2. Count settle records: GET http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1000
3. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --home /tmp/claude-agent-poor --patch krx-all-2761`; note stderr and `echo $?`
4. Re-count settle records
5. Fund the identity: `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js chain fund <new address> 30` (local genesis transfer)
6. Re-run the same `run` command from step 3

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints 'address     0x…', 'publicKey   …', 'home        /tmp/claude-agent-poor'; file /tmp/claude-agent-poor/identity.json is created with mode 0600
- Step 3: the agent reaches step [3] (prints '    402 Payment Required: 25 AIN → …') and '[4] paying (AIN transfer on chain) and retrying with the proof', then fails with stderr 'agent failed: agent 0x<new> holds 0 AIN < price 25 (fund it: ngram chain fund 0x<new>)'; exit code 1; no AIN transfer is sent and no X-PAYMENT retry happens
- Settle count unchanged after the failed run (the seller only records a settlement on a valid X-PAYMENT)
- `chain fund` prints '✓ funded 0x<new> with 30 AIN  tx 0x…  balance now 30 AIN'
- Step 6 succeeds: '    settled: tx 0x…', 'result: SUCCESS — the 402 purchase loop completed', exit code 0; the new address's balance is 5 AIN afterwards (gas-free local chain)

**Evidence**

- `packages/agent/src/agent.ts payFor() ain-transfer branch (balance check message)`
- `packages/agent/src/identity.ts loadIdentity() (creates identity.json, mode 0o600)`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/chain.ts chainFund() output`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts settlePayment()`

### AZ-075 - Reject forged X-PAYMENT proofs: unknown tx hash and a real transfer that did not go to the seller

**Goal:** A seller never releases the manifest for a payment that cannot be verified on the AIN chain as a transfer to the seller.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- Seller node http://localhost:3402 and chain http://localhost:8081 up; krx-all-2761 LISTED; node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)
- A real tx hash of a transfer whose recipient is NOT the seller: pick any non-seller address OTHER (e.g. node-b's from `curl -s http://localhost:3403/api/chain`, or the agent's `keys` output), run `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js chain fund $OTHER 1` and copy the printed 'tx 0x…' (genesis → OTHER)
- Note the newest settle record: GET http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1

**Steps**

1. Build a forged payload: `P=$(printf '{"scheme":"ain-transfer","network":"ain:local","txHash":"0x%064d","from":"%s","to":"0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5","amount":"25","nonce":"deadbeef"}' 1 $OTHER | base64 -w0)`
2. `time curl -s -i -H "x-payment: $P" http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`
3. Build a wrong-recipient payload with the chain-fund tx hash: `P2=$(printf '{"scheme":"ain-transfer","network":"ain:local","txHash":"<fund tx hash>","from":"0x00ADEc28B6a845a085e03591bE7550dd68673C1C","to":"0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5","amount":"25","nonce":"deadbeef"}' | base64 -w0)` then `curl -s -i -H "x-payment: $P2" http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`
4. Send garbage: `curl -s -i -H 'x-payment: %%%not-base64%%%' http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`
5. Send an unsupported scheme: `curl -s -i -H "x-payment: $(echo -n '{"scheme":"paypal"}' | base64 -w0)" http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761`
6. GET http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1 and compare with the record seen before the test

**Expected**

- Step 2: HTTP 402 {"error":"transfer not found / not executed"} after roughly 6 seconds (the seller retries the chain lookup 5 times with 1.2 s pauses); no x-payment-tx-hash header
- Step 3: HTTP 402 {"error":"transfer recipient <OTHER address> is not the seller"} (recipient parsed from the on-chain /transfer/<from>/<to>/<key>/value ref)
- Step 4: HTTP 402 {"error":"missing or malformed X-PAYMENT"}
- Step 5: HTTP 402 {"error":"unsupported scheme paypal"}
- No new settle record, no new 'trade' event, and krx-all-2761 `downloads` unchanged

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/market.ts settlePayment() ain-transfer branch (verifyTransfer retry loop, recipient and value checks, 'unsupported scheme')`
- `packages/core/src/ain-ledger.ts verifyTransfer() (regex /transfer/from/to/key/value, is_executed)`
- `packages/core/src/x402.ts decodePayload()`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id → HttpError(402, out.error)`

### AZ-076 - Reject a replayed X-PAYMENT (payment already used) and ignore stale nonces in the ain-transfer scheme

**Goal:** A tx hash that already bought a manifest cannot be replayed to obtain another manifest or a second download token.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- At least one settle record with scheme 'ain-transfer' AND seller = node-a exists (from the end-to-end agent run or the one-line `use` scenario); take patch_id, tx_hash and buyer from step 1 — never from older notes, the chain is re-seeded on fresh restarts. The settle ledger is shared by every node in the cluster, so the NEWEST ain-transfer record is not necessarily node-a's: replaying a sale another node made is a different scenario (node-a answers 409 'not sold here' for a patch it does not sell)
- Seller node http://localhost:3402 up; node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. Fetch an existing settlement NODE-A SOLD: `curl -s 'http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1000' | python3 -c 'import json,sys;A="0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5";b=next(r["body"] for r in json.load(sys.stdin)["records"] if r["body"]["scheme"]=="ain-transfer" and r["body"]["seller"]==A);print(b["patch_id"],b["tx_hash"],b["buyer"])'`
2. Build a replay payload with that tx hash: `R=$(printf '{"scheme":"ain-transfer","network":"ain:local","txHash":"<tx_hash>","from":"<buyer>","to":"0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5","amount":"25","nonce":"000000000000000000000000"}' | base64 -w0)`
3. `curl -s -i -H "x-payment: $R" http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/<patch_id>` (same patch the tx paid for)
4. `curl -s -i -H "x-payment: $R" http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761` (different patch, same tx; use krx-all-2761-ep12 if the tx already belonged to krx-all-2761)
5. Compare settle count and the target patch's downloads before/after

**Expected**

- Step 3: HTTP 402 {"error":"payment already used"} — the seller's payments_seen table keys on tx_hash and is checked before the transfer is verified, so nonce and amount values are irrelevant for ain-transfer
- Step 4: HTTP 402 {"error":"payment already used"} — a tx cannot be re-used to buy a different patch either
- No new settle record, no new download token, downloads unchanged
- Note for the local-credit scheme (not offered on this AIN demo): the nonce is single-use and a second use answers 402 {"error":"unknown or expired nonce"} (store.takeNonce marks used=1; nonces expire after 10 minutes)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/market.ts settlePayment() (store.paymentSeen(txHash) → 'payment already used'; local-credit takeNonce → 'unknown or expired nonce')`
- `packages/node/src/store.ts (payments_seen table, nonces table takeNonce)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id`

### AZ-077 - Follow supersede marks on a keyword search, and refuse an explicitly requested superseded id

**Goal:** An agent searching by question is steered to the newest verified version, while an explicit request for an outdated version is refused rather than silently paid for.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** agent - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Cluster up; krx-all-2761 LISTED, pixelplus-087600 / krx-all-2761-ep6 SUPERSEDED (agent `catalog --status SUPERSEDED` shows attest 2/2 for them); krx-all-2761 not loaded in the shared model
- Node 24 on PATH; agent identity funded with ≥ 25 AIN (`node packages/cli/dist/bin.js chain fund <agent address> 30`)
- Note the settle count before: GET http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1000

**Steps**

1. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --question "Pixelplus ticker code" --json` (no --patch: keyword search over id/name/description/schema/topic) and read the JSON
2. Re-run the same command without `--json` and read the step [2] lines
3. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --patch krx-all-2761-ep6`; capture stderr and `echo $?`
4. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --patch krx-all-2761-ep6 --follow-latest`; capture stderr
5. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --question "quantum chromodynamics lattice" --patch ""`; capture stderr
6. Compare the settle count

**Expected**

- Step 1: the keyword search matches 'Pixelplus ticker code (single fact)' (pixelplus-087600, SUPERSEDED), follows its supersede mark and buys the newest LISTED version instead: JSON patch_id='krx-all-2761', amount='25', scheme='ain-transfer', success=true, after starting with 087600
- Step 2 shows the log line '    pixelplus-087600 is superseded by krx-all-2761 (newer patch on the same benchmark) → switching' between '[2] searching the catalog (ledger anchors + verification quorum)' and '    candidate: krx-all-2761 …' (a second 25 AIN purchase is made; '    body already present — download skipped')
- Step 3: with an explicit --patch the id is honoured as-is; after step [2] the run stops with stderr 'agent failed: patch krx-all-2761-ep6 is SUPERSEDED, not LISTED — refusing to buy'; exit 1; no payment, no settle record
- Step 4: same refusal as step 3 — the `--follow-latest` flag is accepted by yargs but bin.ts does not forward it into AgentOptions (dead flag) — report as a defect (expected per the option's help text: switch to krx-all-2761)
- Step 5: no catalog entry matches; stderr 'agent failed: no listed patch matches "quantum chromodynamics lattice"'; exit 1
- Settle count increased by exactly 2 (steps 1 and 2 only)

**Evidence**

- `packages/agent/src/agent.ts pickPatch() (keyword score over id/name/description/schema/topic_path, resolveSupersedes log line), runAgent() 'is SUPERSEDED, not LISTED — refusing to buy', 'no listed patch matches'`
- `packages/agent/src/bin.ts run options (--question, --patch, --follow-latest declared but not passed into opts)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/catalog?status=LISTED,SUPERSEDED (superseded_by)`

### AZ-078 - Skip the purchase when the model already answers correctly, and check the --max-price budget guard

**Goal:** An agent never pays for knowledge the running model already has, and an operator can see whether the budget flag actually protects the wallet.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** agent - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Cluster up; vLLM at http://localhost:8000; node-a operator logged in from the CLI (`A="node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home $HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a"`); krx-all-2761 LISTED and not loaded (GET /api/runtime applied = [])
- Node 24 on PATH; a fresh unfunded agent home path e.g. /tmp/claude-agent-budget (must not exist yet)
- Note the settle count before: GET http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1000

**Steps**

1. Run `$A patch apply krx-all-2761` (node-a authored it, so the body is local and no purchase is needed) and confirm `curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/runtime` lists krx-all-2761 in applied
2. Run `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --patch krx-all-2761 --json` (default prompt '종목코드 픽셀플러스 ', expect 087600) and `echo $?`
3. Run `$A patch remove krx-all-2761` and confirm applied is [] again
4. Run `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --home /tmp/claude-agent-budget --patch krx-all-2761 --max-price 10`; capture the log, stderr and `echo $?`
5. Compare the settle count

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints `✓ applied krx-all-2761: …`; /api/runtime applied contains {patch_id:'krx-all-2761', …}
- Step 2: the agent stops after the knowledge check — log (visible without --json) '    current answer: "087600…"  → correct — nothing to buy'; JSON already_known=true, success=true, patch_id=null, scheme=null, tx_hash=null, applied=false, steps has only the [0]/[1] lines; exit 0; no 402 request is made and node-a's `logs --kind buy` shows nothing new
- Step 3 prints `✓ removed krx-all-2761: …`
- Step 4: the budget is NOT enforced — bin.ts does not forward `--max-price` into AgentOptions, so instead of 'price 25 AIN exceeds --max-price 10 — refusing to buy (use --max-price to raise the budget)' the agent proceeds to step [3]/[4] and only fails because the fresh identity is unfunded: stderr 'agent failed: agent 0x… holds 0 AIN < price 25 (fund it: ngram chain fund 0x…)', exit 1 — report the ignored --max-price flag as a defect (the unfunded home keeps the test safe)
- Settle count unchanged after both runs

**Evidence**

- `packages/agent/src/agent.ts runAgent() step [1] ('correct — nothing to buy', already_known/success early return), maxPrice check text, payFor() balance check`
- `packages/agent/src/bin.ts run handler (opts object omits maxPrice and followLatest)`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/patch.ts patchApply/patchRemove`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/patches/:id/apply, GET /api/runtime`

### AZ-079 - Refuse to buy when the seller offers no payment scheme the agent is allowed to use (--pay local-credit on an AIN node)

**Goal:** A policy-restricted agent never pays with a scheme its operator did not authorize.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** agent - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Seller node http://localhost:3402 runs the AIN ledger (GET /api/info → ledger.kind='ain', currency 'AIN'); its 402 only offers scheme 'ain-transfer'
- Node 24 on PATH; krx-all-2761 LISTED and not loaded in the shared model (so the default knowledge check fails and the agent proceeds); agent balance ≥ 25 AIN for step 2

2. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --patch krx-all-2761 --pay ain-transfer --json` (explicit allowed scheme)
3. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --patch krx-all-2761 --pay paypal`

**Expected**

- Step 1: after '[3] requesting the resource → GET http://localhost:3402/x402/patch/krx-all-2761' the agent exits with stderr 'agent failed: 402 without a usable payment requirement (offered: ain-transfer)'; exit code 1; no settle record is added
- Step 2: purchase completes with JSON scheme='ain-transfer', amount='25', success=true

### AZ-080 - Detect a tampered or corrupted patch body by sha256 before applying it to the model

**Goal:** An agent never loads knowledge whose bytes differ from the hash anchored on the ledger, even if the file came from its own cache or an arbitrary peer.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** agent - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Cluster and serving API up; Node 24 on PATH; agent identity funded (≥ 50 AIN for two purchases) — the sha check happens after payment
- A scratch agent home, e.g. /tmp/claude-agent-tamper, seeded with the default identity so funds are available: `mkdir -p /tmp/claude-agent-tamper/patches && cp ~/.ngram-agent/identity.json /tmp/claude-agent-tamper/`
- krx-all-2761 not loaded in the shared model

**Steps**

1. Plant a corrupt cached body under the expected name: `head -c 1048576 /dev/urandom > /tmp/claude-agent-tamper/patches/57c9346349afd6fa1b0475340b9b041ee0027753c05463d4bb204a5a4726c642.npz`
2. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3402 --home /tmp/claude-agent-tamper --patch krx-all-2761`; capture the log, stderr and exit code
3. Delete the corrupt file and re-run the same command

**Expected**

- Step 2 log reaches '[5] manifest sha256 <16 hex>… matches · body sha256 57c9346349afd6fa… (same as the on-ledger anchor)' then '    body already present — download skipped', then aborts with stderr 'agent failed: sha256 mismatch after download: <sha256 of the random file>'; exit code 1
- No '[6] loading into the running model (no restart)' or '    answer with knowledge:' line is printed — the corrupt body never reaches scripts/patch.py (the node's own /api/runtime applied list is [] regardless, since the agent applies directly)
- The manifest itself passed its own check (x-content-sha256 equals sha256 of the response text and manifest.patch_sha256 equals the on-ledger anchor); only the body failed — the 25 AIN payment for this run was still settled
- Step 3: the agent downloads the real body ('    received 347.8 MB from http://localhost:3402/p2p/blob/57c9346349afd6fa…'), prints '    sha256 == on-ledger anchor hash — integrity holds no matter which peer served it' and completes with exit 0

**Evidence**

- `packages/agent/src/agent.ts runAgent() step [5] (manifest hash mismatch / 'manifest sha256 differs from the on-ledger anchor' / existsSync skip / sha256File → 'sha256 mismatch after download')`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /x402/patch/:id sets x-content-sha256 = sha256Hex(manifest text)`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts issueManifest() (patch_sha256 from the anchor)`

### AZ-081 - Write and read back the on-chain access receipt after a node-side purchase (ainize use / POST buy)

**Goal:** A buyer's entitlement is provable on the AIN chain at the path ain-js hasAccess() reads, independent of the seller's database.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- node-c (http://localhost:3404; address from `curl -s http://localhost:3404/api/chain`, currently 0x199Ff87086b81415Cd1402217FFBA3135A51bce9) has an operator session in ~/.ngram-cluster/node-c/cli.json (or log in: `NGRAM_HOME=$HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-c node packages/cli/dist/bin.js login --password <node-c operator password>`) and holds ≥ 25 AIN
- krx-all-2761 LISTED and not yet purchased by node-c: GET http://localhost:3404/api/patches/krx-all-2761 shows purchased=false (has_body may already be true because node-c is a verifier)
- entry_id = GET http://localhost:3402/api/patches/krx-all-2761 anchor.entry_id; node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5); Node 24 on PATH

**Steps**

1. `NGRAM_HOME=$HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-c node packages/cli/dist/bin.js use krx-all-2761 --no-apply` (one-line consumer path; equivalent API: POST http://localhost:3404/api/patches/krx-all-2761/buy with body {"apply":false} and the operator cookie/Bearer token)
2. Read the printed step list and the tx hash
3. Read the receipt on-chain: `curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8081/json-rpc -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"ain_get","params":{"protoVer":"1.0.0","type":"GET_VALUE","ref":"/apps/knowledge/access/<node-c address>"}}'`
4. `NGRAM_HOME=$HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-c node packages/cli/dist/bin.js wallet`
5. Repeat step 1 once more
6. For comparison, after an `ainize-agent run` query the same ref for the agent address (`/apps/knowledge/access/<agent address>`)

**Expected**

- Step 1 prints '✓ bought krx-all-2761 for 25 (ain-transfer)  tx 0x…' followed by timed steps: 'quorum     2 attestation(s) ≥ quorum 2', '402       Payment Required: 25 AIN → 0xF7A9dE499… (ain-transfer)', 'pay       AIN transfer tx 0x…', 'settled   seller confirmed; manifest sha256 …', 'download  body already present; sha256 matches on-ledger anchor' (or '… MB from http://localhost:3402; sha256 matches on-ledger anchor'), 'receipt   on-chain access receipt written (/apps/knowledge/access/…, tx 0x…)', then '  body: /home/comcom/.ngram-cluster/node-c/data/blobs/57c93463….npz' and '✓ downloaded — load with: ainize patch apply krx-all-2761'
- Step 3 returns a child keyed '<node-a address>_finance|krx_<entry_id>' with {seller:'<node-a address>', topic_path:'finance/krx', entry_id:'<entry_id>', amount:'25', currency:'AIN', tx_hash:<same tx>, accessed_at:<ms>} (node-c's access path holds only the receipts node-c itself wrote)
- Step 4 wallet shows 'ledger  ain · ain:local', 'balance <previous − 25> AIN', 'purchases' incremented by 1
- Step 5 prints '✓ krx-all-2761 is already on this node (purchased)' and 'try it: ainize chat krx-all-2761 "your question"' — no second payment, no new receipt
- Step 6: the standalone ainize-agent does NOT write an access receipt (no key under /apps/knowledge/access/<agent address>); its entitlement is only the seller's settle record — expected per current code (recordAccess is called only from Market.buy)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/market.ts buy() (steps quorum/402/pay/settled/download/receipt; ledger.recordAccess only for AinLedger + ain-transfer)`
- `packages/core/src/ain-ledger.ts recordAccess()/hasAccess() (/apps/knowledge/access/$buyer/{owner}_{topicKey}_{entryId})`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/patch.ts patchUse(), patchBuy() output; packages/cli/src/commands/branch.ts wallet()`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/patches/:id/buy, GET /api/me/wallet`
- `packages/agent/src/agent.ts (no recordAccess call)`

### AZ-082 - Split the price along lineage when the source knowledge has a different author (royalty share 0.3)

**Goal:** When an agent buys derived knowledge, the original knowledge creator is paid automatically on-chain without the buyer doing anything extra.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** x402 - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- GET http://localhost:3402/api/info shows royalty_share=0.3; quorum=2; addresses from GET /api/nodes (currently node-a 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5, node-c 0x199Ff87086b81415Cd1402217FFBA3135A51bce9)
- Fixture: node-c (roles verifier+serving, seller of its own patches via http://localhost:3404/x402/patch/<id>) publishes a derived patch whose parent is authored by node-a. Log node-c in first (`NGRAM_HOME=$HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-c node packages/cli/dist/bin.js login --password <pw>`), then: `NGRAM_HOME=$HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-c node packages/cli/dist/bin.js patch publish /mnt/newdata/qwen3.8/results/train-fact/픽셀플러스.npz --id qa-royalty-child --name 'QA royalty child' --model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next --benchmark '{"schema":"qa-royalty-child","queries":1,"format":["template"],"samples":[{"prompt":"종목코드 픽셀플러스 ","expect":"087600"}]}' --price 10 --parents krx-all-2761 --topic finance/krx --announce` (distinct schema so it never supersedes krx-all-2761; duplicate sha256 is allowed); wait until node-a and node-b verifiers attest (GET http://localhost:3404/api/patches/qa-royalty-child → status LISTED, passed 2/2; allow several minutes)
- Agent identity funded with ≥ 10 AIN; Node 24 on PATH; no patch loaded in the shared model (otherwise the agent returns already_known without buying)
- Record node-a's balance: GET http://localhost:3402/api/chain → balance

**Steps**

1. `curl -s http://localhost:3404/x402/patch/qa-royalty-child` and read requirements[0].payTo and maxAmountRequired
2. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js run --market http://localhost:3404 --patch qa-royalty-child --expect 087600 --json`
3. GET http://localhost:3404/api/ledger?kind=settle&limit=1 and read body.royalty
4. GET http://localhost:3404/api/events?kind=royalty&limit=1
5. GET http://localhost:3402/api/chain (node-a balance) and `NGRAM_HOME=$HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a node packages/cli/dist/bin.js wallet`
6. Contrast: GET http://localhost:3402/api/ledger?kind=settle and look at a krx-all-2761 settlement's royalty

**Expected**

- Step 1: payTo=<node-c address> (node-c is the seller of its own patch), maxAmountRequired='10', asset 'AIN'
- Step 2: agent pays 10 AIN to node-c; the non-JSON log would read '    settled: tx 0x…  {"settled":true,"tx":"0x…","royalty":{"<node-c address>":"7","<node-a address>":"3"}}'; JSON success=true, amount='10'
- Step 3 settle body: seller=<node-c>, buyer=<agent>, amount='10', royalty {node-c:'7', node-a:'3'} (pool = 10 × 0.3 = 3 split across 1 ancestor author; lineage walk follows anchor.parents)
- Step 4: event kind 'royalty' with message 'paid 3 AIN royalty to 0xF7A9dE499… (<tx prefix>)' on patch qa-royalty-child (best-effort on-chain transfer executed by the seller node)
- Step 5: node-a's AIN balance increased by 3; node-a wallet lists the row under 'royalties received' with PATCH qa-royalty-child, AMOUNT 3 (API /api/me/wallet royalties[] has {patch_id:'qa-royalty-child', amount:'3'})
- Step 6: krx-all-2761 settlements carry royalty {node-a:'25'} only — parent krx-all-2761-ep12 has the same author, so the ancestor share folds back into the seller and no 'royalty' event is emitted

**Evidence**

- `packages/core/src/catalog.ts royaltySplit() (ancestor authors via anchor.parents, pool = amount × share)`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts settlePayment() (royalty map in settlement, ledger.transfer per ancestor, 'royalty' event), announce() gateway_url = this node`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/me/wallet (royalties = settlements where seller !== me and royalty[me]), GET /api/info royalty_share`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/patch.ts patchPublish (--parents, --announce); packages/cli/src/commands/branch.ts wallet() 'royalties received'`

### AZ-083 - Meter live-test hits through POST /api/chat and read them back as usage events with a per-visitor quota

**Goal:** Every patched answer served to an automated caller is recorded as a billable 'hit' with latency data, and anonymous callers are capped at 20 per hour.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** api - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- Seller node http://localhost:3402 holds the body of krx-all-2761 and its runtime is available (GET /api/chat/patches lists krx-all-2761; runtime.available=true)
- Caller is NOT logged in as operator (no ngram_session cookie) so the visitor key is 'ip:<addr>'; no anonymous live test from this IP in the last hour
- Note the latest usage event seq: GET http://localhost:3402/api/events?kind=usage&limit=1

**Steps**

1. `curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3402/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"patch_id":"krx-all-2761","mode":"compare","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"종목코드 픽셀플러스 "}],"max_tokens":16}'`
2. GET http://localhost:3402/api/events?kind=usage&limit=1 and GET http://localhost:3402/api/patches/krx-all-2761/events?limit=1
3. `curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3402/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"patch_id":"krx-all-2761","mode":"base","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"What is the ticker of Pixelplus?"}]}'` then re-read the newest usage event
4. Repeat step 1 in a loop until remaining_quota reaches 0, then send one more request (at most 21 requests in the same hour from the same IP; the base-only call of step 3 also consumed one try)
5. `curl -s -X POST http://localhost:3402/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"patch_id":"krx-all-2761","mode":"compare","messages":[]}'`

**Expected**

- Step 1: HTTP 200 JSON with patch_id='krx-all-2761', mode='compare', base.content (pre-patch answer), patched.content containing '087600', benchmark_hit=true (prompt matches a benchmark sample; expected 087600), applied_ms > 0, was_applied=false, model='Qwen3.8-Flash-Next', quota_limit=20 and remaining_quota one less than before
- Step 2: newest event has kind='usage', patch_id='krx-all-2761', message 'live test krx-all-2761 (compare) by ip:<addr>: patched hit=true', data {visitor:'ip:<addr>', mode:'compare', hit:true, base_ms, patched_ms, applied_ms}; the same event appears under /api/patches/krx-all-2761/events
- Step 3: mode 'base' returns patched=null and benchmark_hit=null; the usage event message ends with 'base only' (no hit is metered when no patched answer was served); a free-form question in compare mode yields hit=null ('not auto-scored')
- Step 4: when the 20-per-hour window is exhausted the request answers HTTP 429 {"error":"quota_chat: free live-test quota exhausted for this hour — buy the patch or run your own node","quota_reset":<epoch ms>} — quota_reset is the measured end of this visitor's window (window start + 3,600,000 ms), which is what the live-test page prints in place of a Retry button that could do nothing — and no usage event is written for it (the quota is checked before the runtime runs)
- Step 5: HTTP 400 {"error":"invalid request","issues":[…]} (messages must have 1–24 items); no usage event
- `patch_id` stays valid for a single knowledge; POST /api/chat also accepts `patch_ids` (1–3 ids, exactly one of the two fields) and then returns `patch_ids`, `applied[]` and one usage event per knowledge — see AZ-101

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/chat (chatQuota check, 429 message, remaining_quota/quota_limit), GET /api/events, GET /api/patches/:id/events, zod schema`
- `packages/node/src/market.ts chat() ('usage' event with visitor/mode/hit/base_ms/patched_ms/applied_ms), chatQuota() (20 per 3600000 ms)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts 'chat.dev.body' ('every patched answer is metered as a usage hit'), 'chat.hit.unknown'`

### AZ-084 - Inspect the agent's identity, catalog view and credit balance with the keys / catalog / balance subcommands

**Goal:** An operator scripting the agent can discover its address to fund, see which knowledge is buyable, and machine-read everything as JSON.

**Priority:** P2 - **Area:** agent - **Automation:** cli

**Preconditions**

- Cluster up with the demo catalog fully verified (krx-all-2761 LISTED, three SUPERSEDED with attest 2/2); Node 24 on PATH (`export PATH=$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH`); working directory /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace
- Default agent home ~/.ngram-agent exists (created automatically on first use)

**Steps**

1. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js keys` and `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js keys --reveal --json`; `ls -l ~/.ngram-agent/identity.json`
2. `NGRAM_AGENT_HOME=/tmp/claude-agent-env node packages/agent/dist/bin.js keys`
3. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js catalog --market http://localhost:3402` then `… catalog --market http://localhost:3402 --status LISTED,SUPERSEDED --json | python3 -c 'import json,sys;print([(e["anchor"]["id"],e["status"],e["superseded_by"],e["quorum_ok"]) for e in json.load(sys.stdin)])'`
4. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js catalog --market http://localhost:3402 --status REJECTED`
5. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js balance --market http://localhost:3402` and `… balance --market http://localhost:3402 --json`; `… balance --market http://localhost:3402 --initial 5`
6. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js balance --market http://localhost:9999`
7. `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js` (no command) and `node packages/agent/dist/bin.js --help`

**Expected**

- keys prints three rows 'address     0x<40 hex>', 'publicKey   <128 hex>', 'home        /home/comcom/.ngram-agent'; --reveal --json adds "privateKey"; identity.json has mode -rw------- (0600)
- With NGRAM_AGENT_HOME set, keys reports home '/tmp/claude-agent-env' and a different, newly generated address (the env var overrides ~/.ngram-agent; --home overrides both)
- Default catalog prints exactly the LISTED rows: 'krx-all-2761             LISTED       270053 rows  25 AIN  attest 2/2  KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)'; the JSON query lists krx-all-2761 (LISTED, [], True) plus pixelplus-087600, krx-all-2761-ep12, krx-all-2761-ep6 (SUPERSEDED, ['krx-all-2761', …], True)
- `--status REJECTED` prints '(no patches)' and exits 0
- balance prints '<address>  100 CREDIT (initial credit 100 from http://localhost:3402/api/info; CREDIT = local dev credit of this node, not AIN)'; JSON has {address, balance:100, currency:'CREDIT', initial_credit:100, initial_credit_source:'http://localhost:3402/api/info'}; with --initial 5 the source is 'override' and balance 5. Purchases on this AIN demo are ain-transfer, so they do not reduce CREDIT — AIN funds must be read from the chain (ain_getBalance) or the node's /api/chain
- Unreachable market: stderr 'balance failed: info request failed: …' (or fetch failed), exit code 1
- No command: 'Specify a command. Try `ainize-agent --help`.' with exit 1; --help lists commands run, catalog, balance, keys and global options --market (default http://localhost:3402), --home, --json

**Evidence**

- `packages/agent/src/bin.ts (commands keys/catalog/balance, PROG name, demandCommand message, catalog row format)`
- `packages/agent/src/identity.ts agentHome() (NGRAM_AGENT_HOME), loadIdentity() (0o600)`
- `packages/agent/src/agent.ts fetchCatalog(), creditBalance(), fetchInitialCredit()`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/info (initial_credit), GET /api/catalog status filter`

### AZ-085 - Show a plain error when the node API is unreachable on every public page

**Goal:** A visitor who loses the connection to the node sees an explicit error instead of a blank page, a permanent spinner or a misleading empty state.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** error - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running (scripts/cluster.mjs): web UI at http://localhost:3402
- Chrome with DevTools available
- UI language English (default)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761 and wait until the knowledge picker shows the 4 items
2. Open DevTools > Network and tick 'Offline'
3. Type 'hello' in the box labelled 'Type a question and press Enter (Shift+Enter for a new line)' and press Enter
4. Untick 'Offline' and click the 'Retry' button under the failed turn (stay on the page — the transcript lives in component state)
5. Tick 'Offline' again and click the header link 'Explore knowledge'
6. Still offline, click the header link 'Public record', then 'Network'; then untick 'Offline'

**Expected**

- Step 3: the turn shows a red alert (role=alert) with exactly 'Could not reach the node. Check your connection and try again.' and a 'Retry' button; no raw 'TypeError' text
- Step 4: 'Retry' resends the same prompt and the turn completes with 'Before loading' / 'After loading' bubbles
- Step 5: /explore renders the red alert 'Something went wrong: TypeError: Failed to fetch' (common.error with the transport message); no spinner is left running
- Step 6: /ledger shows the empty box 'No records yet.'; /network renders the cached 'This node' / 'Model server' cards (RTK Query keeps the last /api/info data from the header's poll), the empty 'No connected nodes yet…' state and a spinner under 'Knowledge tracks' — LedgerPage and NetworkPage have no error branch; log as a defect if the team expects an explicit error message there; the header badge 'AI Network' never disappears

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx mapChatError (FETCH_ERROR -> chat.err.network), transcript in component state`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts chat.err.network, chat.turn.retry`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/common.ts common.error`
- `packages/web/src/api/api.ts errorMessage`
- `packages/web/src/pages/ExplorePage.tsx (error branch)`
- `packages/web/src/pages/LedgerPage.tsx (no error branch)`
- `packages/web/src/pages/NetworkPage.tsx (spinner while !info; shared /api/info cache)`

### AZ-086 - Refuse to downgrade to an integrity-only attestation during the 15-minute runtime grace period

**Goal:** A temporary vLLM outage never produces a hash-only attestation that would dilute the 'Verified on the real model' count.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** verification - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- docker container 'flashnext' (vLLM on :8000) is paused (`docker pause flashnext`) and 60 s have passed (Runtime.status() is cached 30 s); unpause it afterwards
- Operator password known for node-a (create it on http://localhost:3402/signing if 'Create the operator password' is shown)
- krx-all-2761 currently shows 'Verified on the real model 2/2' and '0' integrity-only checks; node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. Run: curl -s -c /tmp/ainize.cookies -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"password":"<operator password>"}' http://localhost:3402/api/auth/login
2. Run: curl -s -i -b /tmp/ainize.cookies -X POST http://localhost:3402/api/patches/krx-all-2761/verify
3. Open http://localhost:3402/0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5/krx-all-2761 and click the tab 'Verification'
4. Run: curl -s 'http://localhost:3402/api/events?limit=5&kind=verifier' | head -c 600
5. Run: docker unpause flashnext

**Expected**

- Step 1: HTTP 200 {"ok":true,"token":...} and a Set-Cookie ngram_session
- Step 2: HTTP 500 with body exactly {"error":"runtime unavailable (serving API unreachable) — waiting up to 15 min before hash-only fallback"}; no attestation is returned (the variant 'serving API unreachable (retrying for 15 more min before hash-only fallback)' only occurs when the runtime was reachable but the benchmark run itself failed)
- Step 3: summary still reads 'Run on the real model 2/2' and 'Integrity only 0'; the table still has exactly two rows (node-b, node-c) with Method 'run on the real model' and Accuracy '26/26'; no new row with 'integrity only'
- Step 4: the newest verifier event is the info line 'verifying krx-all-2761 (KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final))'; no attest record was appended to the public record (Public record 'Records' count unchanged)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/verifier.ts RUNTIME_GRACE_MS, verifyOne (graceLeft branches)`
- `packages/node/src/runtime.ts status() (30 s cache, 'serving API unreachable')`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/patches/:id/verify, POST /api/auth/login`
- `packages/web/src/pages/PatchPage.tsx Verification (SummaryRow, isExecuted)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/detail.ts detail.ver.summary_executed, detail.ver.summary_integrity, detail.how.executed`

- Step 4: Korean persists after reload and on every route (landing hero '지식을 AI에 끼우다', chat title '라이브 테스트', ledger title '공개 기록'); the landing page's dark nav has its own toggle that switches back too; each route names itself in the tab ('지식을 AI에 끼우다 · Ainize', '라이브 테스트 · Ainize', '공개 기록 · Ainize', 'KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final) · Ainize', '404. 페이지를 찾을 수 없습니다 · Ainize')

- The Korean landing page renders every heading and both hero CTA labels even on a host with no system Hangul font (fc-list :lang=ko empty): theme.font.display and theme.font.mono both end in 'Apple SD Gothic Neo', 'Noto Sans KR', the webfont index.html already downloads — h1 '지식을 AI에 끼우다' has a non-zero width and its computed font-family contains 'Noto Sans KR'

### AZ-088 - Keep the operator signed in across refresh and new tabs via the session cookie, and sign out cleanly

**Goal:** An operator does not have to re-enter the node password on every reload, and logging out really revokes access to operator pages.

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** signing - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running; node-a at http://localhost:3402
- If /signing shows 'Create the operator password', create one first (min 4 chars, tick 'I agree to the Terms and Policies (required)')

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/signing, enter the operator password under 'Sign in to your node' and click 'Sign in'
2. Press F5 on the resulting page; then open a new tab at http://localhost:3402/account
3. In DevTools > Application > Cookies inspect 'ngram_session'; in the console run document.cookie
4. Open http://localhost:3402/ in the signed-in tab
5. Open the header menu 'node-a ▾' and click 'Log out'; then open http://localhost:3402/dashboard
6. Sign in again from that redirect

**Expected**

- Step 1: redirected to /dashboard with title 'My knowledge'; header shows 'My knowledge' and the menu button 'node-a ▾' instead of 'Sign in'
- Step 2: after refresh the dashboard shows a brief spinner then renders (no bounce to /signing); the new tab renders 'Account settings' directly
- Step 3: cookie ngram_session is HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax, expires in about 30 days; document.cookie does not contain it
- Step 4: the landing page immediately redirects a signed-in operator to /dashboard
- Step 5: after 'Log out' the browser lands on '/' (landing, no redirect loop) and /dashboard now redirects to /signing?next=%2Fdashboard; GET /api/auth/me returns signedIn false
- Step 6: signing in returns to /dashboard (the next parameter is honoured)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/api.ts SESSION_COOKIE, /api/auth/login (httpOnly, sameSite lax, 30 d), /api/auth/logout, /api/auth/me`
- `packages/web/src/auth/AuthContext.tsx (useMeQuery)`

### AZ-089 - Recover automatically after the node process restarts under an open Live test tab

**Goal:** When the seller node dies and comes back, an open browser tab reports the outage in plain language and heals itself without a manual reload.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** error - **Automation:** manual

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running via scripts/cluster.mjs (note: the supervisor only respawns a child that exits within 15 s of being started — a SIGTERM to the long-running node-a is NOT respawned, so the tester restarts it by hand)
- Shell access on the demo machine; `export PATH="$HOME/.local/node/bin:$PATH"; cd /mnt/newdata/ainize/knowledge-marketplace`
- http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761 open in a browser, picker loaded, no operator sign-in

**Steps**

1. In a shell run: ss -ltnp 'sport = :3402' and note the pid of packages/node/dist/bin.js
2. Run: kill <pid>  (SIGTERM)
3. Immediately in the browser click the sample chip '종목코드 픽셀플러스' and press Enter
4. After ~10 s restart node-a: `NGRAM_HOME=$HOME/.ngram-cluster/node-a NGRAM_PORT=3402 setsid nohup node packages/node/dist/bin.js >> ~/.ngram-cluster/node-a/node.log 2>&1 &` (or bounce all three nodes with scripts/cluster-restart.sh)
5. Wait 30 s without reloading; run: curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/info | head -c 200
6. Click 'Retry' under the failed turn
7. Run: curl -s http://localhost:3402/api/runtime | python3 -c "import sys,json;print(json.load(sys.stdin)['applied'])"

**Expected**

- Step 3: while the port is closed the turn fails with 'Could not reach the node. Check your connection and try again.' and a 'Retry' button (connection refused maps to FETCH_ERROR)
- Step 5: /api/info answers 200 again with node.name 'node-a'; the chat page's 20 s poll of /api/chat/patches repopulates 'Knowledge to test' without a reload; header badge 'AI Network' stays
- Step 6: Retry succeeds: 'Before loading' and 'After loading' bubbles appear, the patched bubble shows 'loaded in N.Ns' and '✓ Correct' with expected 087600
- Step 7: applied is [] (the shared table was restored; a stale lock from the killed process is broken by the liveness check in acquireLock)

**Evidence**

- `scripts/cluster.mjs spawnNode (respawn only when the child exited within 15 s of start)`
- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx pollingInterval 20_000, mapChatError`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts chat.err.network, chat.bubble.applied, chat.hit.yes`
- `packages/node/src/runtime.ts acquireLock (holderAlive check)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts GET /api/runtime`

### AZ-091 - Show honest loading states while a 331.7 MB knowledge is loaded, and allow cancelling

**Goal:** A visitor waiting tens of seconds for a compare test always knows what is happening and can bail out.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** perf - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running with vLLM available
- http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761 open (krx-all-2761 is 347,829,014 bytes = 'Size 331.7 MB' in Explore)
- No operator sign-in (visitor quota applies)

**Steps**

1. Note the footer text under the composer (e.g. 'Free tries are limited per hour. No sign-in needed.' or 'Free trial N/20 left this hour')
2. Select 'Compare', click the chip '종목코드 픽셀플러스', press Enter and observe the UI during the request
3. After the answer arrives, read the meta line of the 'After loading' bubble
4. Send the same prompt again and click 'Cancel' / 'Stop waiting' in the strip above the composer within 2 s
5. Read the footer quota text, click 'Retry' under the cancelled turn and read the footer again after it completes

**Expected**

- Step 2: user bubble 'You' appears; both bubbles show three pulsing dots (aria-label 'Generating…') and the note 'Includes loading and unloading — this can take tens of seconds.'; the Send button shows a spinner with 'Waiting for the answer…'; a strip (role=status) reads 'Waiting for the answer — you can cancel if it takes too long.' with a 'Cancel' button; the 'View' radios, 'Enable thinking' and the textarea are disabled; the spinner does not block the picker
- Step 3: the patched bubble header shows 'After loading · reply NNNms · loaded in N.Ns' (applied_ms around 2-4 s for this file) and '✓ Correct'; the base bubble shows 'Before loading · reply N.Ns'
- Step 4: the turn switches to a red alert naming which of the two things happened — 'You stopped waiting. The node had not started this test yet, so no free try was used.' while it was still queued, or 'You stopped waiting, but the test had already started on the shared model, so it still counts as one free try.' once the node had taken the shared lock — with a 'Retry' button; the composer unlocks immediately. The pre-D3 wording 'Request cancelled.' must no longer appear: a wait is not a rejection, and the visitor is told whether it cost them anything
- Step 5: the quota text is unchanged at cancel time either way (no response arrived). What the retry then costs follows the message, and which message appears is a genuine race against the shared lock: cancelled while still QUEUED nothing was ever sent to the model, so only the retry is charged and the footer drops by one; cancelled once it was already RUNNING the node finishes the work and charges it, so cancel + retry drop the footer by two (e.g. 19/20 to 17/20). The 'a cancelled try is always charged' finding this scenario used to record is fixed for the queued case (POST /api/chat answers HTTP 499 and consumes nothing)

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/components/chat/TurnView.tsx pending Dots / PendingNote / QueuePending`
- `packages/web/src/pages/ChatPage.tsx CancelRow, cancel(), inflight abort, quota handling`
- `packages/web/src/components/chat/ChatComposer.tsx loading / locked`
- `packages/node/src/chat-queue.ts ChatQueue.cancel() (free while queued, honest once running) + ChatCancelledError (HTTP 499)`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/chat (quota checked without consuming, consumed only after market.chat resolves), POST /api/chat/cancel`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts chat.queue.cancelled, chat.queue.cancelled_late, chat.bubble.compare_pending, chat.input.sending, chat.bubble.applied`
- `packages/web/src/utils/format.ts bytes()`

### AZ-092 - Keep every page usable at 360 px width without horizontal page scrolling, and the header free of collisions at desktop widths

**Goal:** A visitor on a small phone can reach all navigation items, read lists and scroll wide tables inside their container.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** a11y - **Automation:** manual

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running
- Chrome DevTools device toolbar set to 360 x 740, English UI
- node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/ and inspect the top navigation ('Explore knowledge', 'Live test', 'Node sign-in', language button)
2. Open http://localhost:3402/explore; check the header row (logo, 'AI Network' badge, 'Explore knowledge', 'Live test', 'Network', 'Public record', 'Docs & API', 'Sign in', '한국어') and the list items
3. Open http://localhost:3402/0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5/krx-all-2761, check the stats strip, then open the tab 'Verification' and swipe the table sideways
4. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761, send the chip '종목코드 픽셀플러스'
5. Open http://localhost:3402/ledger and scroll to 'Origin → derivative map'
6. On each page run in the console: document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth
7. Set the viewport to 1440, 1280, 1024 and 960 px on /explore and measure the 'AI Network' ledger badge against the first nav link

**Expected**

- Step 1: the landing nav wraps onto a second line (flex-wrap) and all four controls are tappable
- Step 2: all header items are reachable and the console check is true - NOTE the inner Header nav uses white-space: nowrap without flex-wrap (Header.tsx), so overflow at 360 px is a likely defect: record actual behaviour; Explore items switch to 16 px padding and a 40 px icon; the right-hand price column (e.g. '25 AIN') is hidden below 600 px by design
- Step 3: the seven stats ('Purchases', 'accuracy', 'Memory entries', 'Facts', 'Size', 'Price', 'Revenue') wrap; the verification table scrolls horizontally inside its wrapper while the page itself does not
- Step 4: the picker stacks above the transcript (single column below 960 px) and 'Before loading' / 'After loading' bubbles stack vertically below 600 px; the textarea and 'Send' stay on one row
- Step 5: the SVG map scrolls inside its box (overflow-x auto) and the legend wraps
- Step 6: true on every page (no body-level horizontal scroll)
- Step 7: with the webfonts in place, at 1440, 1280, 1024 and 960 px the 'AI Network' ledger badge and 'Explore knowledge' do not intersect, the logo and the nav share one row and the bar is 81 px tall, and the nav's items fit the width left beside the logo (Home is flex: 0 0 auto, Nav flex: 1 1 auto and the nav items carry 10 px of horizontal padding). While the fallback face is still showing, or below ~900 px, the bar wraps onto a second row instead of overlapping.

### AZ-093 - Operate the Live test and sign-in entirely from the keyboard with visible focus

**Goal:** A keyboard-only user can pick knowledge, send a question with Enter, insert a newline with Shift+Enter and sign in, always seeing where focus is.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** a11y - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running with vLLM available
- Operator password already created for node-a
- Desktop browser, English UI, mouse untouched during the test

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/chat/krx-all-2761 and press Tab repeatedly from the address bar, noting the focus order and the focus ring
2. Tab to the picker item 'KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies — epoch 12' (krx-all-2761-ep12) and press Enter
3. Tab to the 'View' group and press Enter on 'After only'; Tab to 'Enable thinking' and press Space twice
4. Tab into the textarea, type '종목코드', press Shift+Enter, type '픽셀플러스', then press Enter
5. While the answer is pending press Shift+Tab from the textarea and observe; after the answer, Tab to 'Clear conversation' and press Enter
6. Open http://localhost:3402/signing, type a wrong password in the focused 'Operator password' field and press Enter; then type the right password and press Enter

**Expected**

- Step 1: order is 'Ainize home' link → 'Explore knowledge' → 'Live test' → 'Network' → 'Public record' → 'Docs & API' → 'Sign in' → language button → picker items → 'Details →' → sample chips → 'Show N more' → 'Compare' / 'After only' / 'Before only' → 'Enable thinking' → textarea (→ 'Send' only once text is typed; the button is disabled while the textarea is empty); every focused control shows a 2 px purple (#8b3eeb) outline with 2 px offset; the textarea shows a purple border plus pale ring instead
- Step 2: the URL becomes /chat/krx-all-2761-ep12, the item shows 'Selected' and the header changes to that knowledge's name with status 'Newer version available'
- Step 3: 'After only' becomes aria-checked=true; the checkbox toggles and the helper flips between 'Thinking is on — replies are slower' and the default help sentence
- Step 4: Shift+Enter inserts a line break (textarea grows, max 160 px) and Enter sends; a single 'After loading' bubble appears; the composer clears; no submit happens while an IME composition is active
- Step 5: during the request the textarea and Send are disabled and Shift+Tab reaches 'Cancel' (the cancel strip sits above the composer in DOM order); 'Clear conversation' empties the transcript back to 'No questions yet' and then disappears
- Step 6: the password field is auto-focused; wrong password shows the red alert 'wrong password' (server text); the right one signs in and lands on /dashboard ('My knowledge')

**Evidence**

Check your connection and try again.'; the runtime/lock/quota boxes use role=status
- Step 3: role=tablist with five role=tab ('Overview', 'Verification', 'Origins & derivatives', 'Buy', 'History') exposing aria-selected; the status chip 'For sale' has a title tooltip 'On sale as the current version for this topic. Whether it passed verification is what the "Verified" badge beside it says.' (note: tabs have no arrow-key navigation - record as P2 gap)
- Step 4: svg role=img aria-label 'knowledge origin and derivative map'; pagination arrows have aria-label 'previous' / 'next' and text buttons 'First' / 'Last'
- Step 5: no critical axe violations; document any 'button-name' or 'color-contrast' findings with element and page

### AZ-100 - Degrade gracefully when clipboard copy is unavailable or denied

**Goal:** A visitor copying a content hash or node address gets 'Copied' feedback on success and no crash (ideally a visible failure) when the browser blocks the clipboard.

**Priority:** P2 - **Area:** error - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Demo cluster running; Chrome; English UI
- Optionally the machine's LAN IP to open the UI over an insecure (non-localhost) origin
- node-a address = GET /api/info node.address (currently 0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5)

**Steps**

1. Open http://localhost:3402/0xF7A9dE49902C95661AC6556D631e2B60a081A1F5/krx-all-2761, scroll to 'Integrity' and click 'Copy' next to 'Content hash'
2. Paste into the search box on /explore to check the clipboard content, then clear it
3. Back on the detail page open DevTools console and run: Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'clipboard', { value: undefined, configurable: true })
4. Click 'Copy' again and watch the button label and the console
5. Open http://localhost:3402/signing and click 'Copy' next to 'Account address' in the 'This node' box, with the same override applied
6. Optional: open http://<LAN-IP>:3402/... (insecure context) and click 'Copy'

**Expected**

- Step 1: the button label switches to 'Copied' for about 1.5 s and returns to 'Copy'
- Step 2: the pasted text is the 64-hex sha256 starting 57c9346349afd6fa
- Step 4: the click is swallowed silently: label stays 'Copy', no 'Copied', no uncaught exception in the console, the page keeps working (error is caught in CopyButton) - record the missing failure feedback as a P2 UX finding
- Step 5: same behaviour for the address copy on the sign-in page
- Step 6: in an insecure context navigator.clipboard is undefined, so the same silent no-op occurs; the rest of the page is unaffected

**Evidence**

- `packages/web/src/components/ui/Misc.tsx CopyButton (try/catch around navigator.clipboard.writeText, 1500 ms reset)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/common.ts common.copy, common.copied, common.copy_clipboard`
- `packages/web/src/pages/PatchPage.tsx Integrity section CopyButton`
- `packages/web/src/pages/SigningPage.tsx NodeBox CopyButton (op.address)`

## Teach mode (visitor)

### TM-090 - Load up to three knowledges together in one live test, see the overlap warning, and meter one usage event per knowledge

**Goal:** A visitor can tick several knowledges (max 3) in the Live test picker; they load in tick order (the last one wins on overlapping memory entries), the picker warns about the overlap, and the node bills one usage event per knowledge.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- A teach-mode node (this branch) whose GET /api/chat/patches lists at least two knowledges that share memory entries (`overlaps[]` non-empty; on the demo data `krx-all-2761` ∩ `pixelplus-087600` = 2,170 rows) and `runtime.available=true`
- Caller is NOT signed in as operator (visitor quota applies)
- Note the newest usage event: GET /api/events?kind=usage&limit=1

**Steps**

1. Open `/chat`. The picker is titled "Knowledge to load (pick up to 3)" with the help line "They load in the order you tick them. If two overlap, the one ticked last wins."
2. Tick `KRX ticker codes for 2,761 listed companies (final)` then `Pixelplus ticker code (single fact)`; observe the order badges (1, 2), the counter "2/3 selected" and the address bar (`/chat/krx-all-2761,pixelplus-087600`)
3. Tick a third knowledge, then hover a fourth: the remaining checkboxes are disabled with the tooltip "Up to 3 — untick one first."
4. Untick the third one, send the sample question `픽셀플러스 종목코드 알려줘. 숫자만.` in Compare mode
5. `curl -s -X POST <node>/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"patch_ids":["krx-all-2761","pixelplus-087600"],"mode":"compare","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"종목코드 픽셀플러스 "}],"max_tokens":16}'`
6. GET /api/events?kind=usage&limit=2
7. `curl -s -X POST <node>/api/chat -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"patch_id":"krx-all-2761","patch_ids":["pixelplus-087600"],"mode":"base","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"x"}]}'` and the same with four ids in `patch_ids`

**Expected**

- Step 2: an info alert "KRX ticker codes … and Pixelplus ticker code … overlap on 2,170 memory entries — Pixelplus ticker code …, ticked last, wins." (`chat.picker.overlap_pair`); both rows show "Loads 1." / "Loads 2."; the transcript header reads "2 knowledges loaded together" with the two names numbered
- Step 4: the "After loading 2" bubble lists both knowledges with their load times ("krx-all-2761: loaded in …", "pixelplus-087600: loaded in …") and the per-knowledge ✓/✗ chips; the answer contains 087600
- Step 5: HTTP 200 with `patch_id='krx-all-2761'`, `patch_ids=['krx-all-2761','pixelplus-087600']`, `applied=[{patch_id,applied_ms,was_applied}×2]`, `applied_ms` = the sum, `benchmark_hits` keyed by both ids, `benchmark_hit` = OR of them, `base`/`patched` as before; the lock label seen by other clients during the call is `chat:krx-all-2761+pixelplus-087600`
- Step 6: two new usage events, one per patch_id, each with `data.patch_ids` = both ids and `data.position` 1/2; the node's shared table is back to its previous state afterwards (GET /api/runtime `applied` unchanged)
- Step 7: HTTP 400 for both (exactly one of `patch_id` / `patch_ids`; at most 3 ids)

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/market.ts chat() (patchIds[], exclusive('chat:<id1>+<id2>'), applyRaw in list order, restore in reverse, one usage event per patch), chatOverlaps()`
- `packages/node/src/api.ts POST /api/chat (patch_id | patch_ids refine), GET /api/chat/patches (applied[], overlaps[])`
- `packages/web/src/components/chat/KnowledgePicker.tsx (checkboxes, order badges, overlap alert data-testid=chat-overlap)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts chat.picker.multi_title, chat.picker.multi_help, chat.picker.overlap_pair, chat.picker.max, chat.head.multi, chat.bubble.patched_multi`
- `packages/cli/src/commands/chat.ts parsePatchIds(), renderChat() (per-knowledge load order and markers); ainize chat --patch a,b`

### TM-091 - Show the contamination banner when the operator keeps knowledge loaded for everyone

**Goal:** A visitor understands that "Before loading" is not the pristine base model when the operator has pinned knowledge into the serving model.

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** chat - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Teach-mode node with `runtime.available=true`; operator credentials for it
- GET /api/chat/patches returns `applied: []`

**Steps**

1. Open `/chat` as a visitor: no orange banner above the list
2. As operator: `POST /api/patches/pixelplus-087600/apply` (or Manage → Load into model)
3. Reload `/chat` as a visitor (or wait for the 20 s poll)
4. Tick `Pixelplus ticker code (single fact)` and send `픽셀플러스 종목코드 알려줘. 숫자만.` in Compare mode
5. As operator: `POST /api/patches/pixelplus-087600/remove`

**Expected**

- Step 2: GET /api/chat/patches → `applied: ['pixelplus-087600']`
- Step 3: warning banner "This node also has Pixelplus ticker code (single fact) loaded for everyone, so "Before loading" already includes it." (`chat.picker.contaminated`, data-testid=chat-contaminated) and an "Always loaded" chip on that row
- Step 4: the response has `applied[0].was_applied=true`; in Compare mode the node still removes it for the "Before" answer and puts it back afterwards (GET /api/runtime `applied` still lists it); the bubble shows "was already loaded" when the After answer needed no re-load (After-only mode)
- Step 5: the banner disappears on the next poll

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/market.ts pinnedPatchIds(); chat() restore step re-asserts pinned knowledge after an overlapping removal`
- `packages/web/src/components/chat/KnowledgePicker.tsx (applied → Alert $tone=warning, Pinned chip)`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/chat.ts chat.picker.contaminated, chat.picker.always_loaded, chat.bubble.already_applied`

### AZ-103 - Teach drawer from a wrong answer → basket persists across reload

**Goal:** A visitor corrects a wrong reply from Live test and the correction stays in the lesson basket after a reload (spec §13.2 AZ-092, §5.4, §5.5).

**Priority:** P0 - **Area:** teach - **Automation:** e2e

**Preconditions**

- Teach-mode node with `teach.enabled: true`, `backend: 'stub'` and `stubOffline: true` (dev node :3412: `node packages/cli/dist/bin.js --home ~/.ngram-teach/node-t start`, operator password teach-pass) — no GPU job is ever started
- runtime.available=true for the one completion (AZ-103 waits for the serving model)

**Steps**

1. Open /chat/pixelplus-087600?teach=1 as a visitor
2. Choose 'Before only', ask '픽셀플러스 (<tag>) 종목코드는?' and click 'Teach the right answer' under the reply
3. Click 'Add to lesson' with an empty answer, then type 087600, an alternative phrasing, and add
4. Add a second correction whose question already contains the answer (e.g. '종목코드 087600은 픽셀플러스인가요?' → 픽셀플러스)
5. Reload the page

**Expected**

- Banner 'Wrong answer? Click "Teach the right answer" under any reply and the model learns it. No account needed.'; header item 'Teach'; basket 'Your lesson (0 of 8)' / 'No corrections yet.'; policy line 'Teaching on this node: open'; 'Train this lesson' disabled
- Drawer 'Teach the right answer' with the question prefilled, 'The model said' read-only; empty answer → alert 'Please type the right answer.'
- Basket shows 'Your lesson (1 of 8)' then '(2 of 8)' with question + answer per item; 'Train this lesson' enabled
- After reload the basket still shows 2 corrections (localStorage key ainize.teach.basket.<stack>)

**Evidence**

- Teacher page: earnings.owed = earnings.paid (local-credit settles are credited by the record itself, no payouts row), sales 1, item status paid

### AZ-114 - Lineage: knowledge built on a taught lesson pays the data provider their slice (pass 1b)

**Goal:** When someone publishes knowledge whose parent is a taught lesson, the lesson’s data provider receives their share of the lineage pool (spec §13.2 AZ-103, §7.3).

**Steps**

1. Buy the child knowledge
2. Inspect settle.royalty

**Expected**

- Pass 1: 30% lineage pool to the parent's author (the node); pass 1b: that slice is shared 70/30 with the parent's contributor; pass 2: the child's own contributors (if any) carve the seller remainder; zero-valued lines omitted

### AZ-117 - Trainer slot busy (pgrep returns an operator job) → job stays QUEUED with the 'fully booked' copy; frees when the job ends

**Goal:** Visitor lessons never compete with the operator’s own training: the worker leases the trainer slot and waits (spec §13.2 AZ-106, §8.1, §8.5).

**Priority:** P1 - **Area:** teach - **Automation:** api

**Preconditions**

- Gradient backend node whose `docker exec flashtrain pgrep -f train/` finds a running operator job (or the unit test's fake exec)

**Steps**

1. Queue a lesson
2. Read GET /api/teach/jobs/<id> and the card
3. End the operator job

**Expected**

- status QUEUED with blocked='slot'; card copy 'The trainer is fully booked right now — your lesson is queued…'; GET /api/teach/policy trainer='busy'
- When pgrep no longer matches (and nvidia-smi shows ≥ minFreeGpuMb on GPUs 4–6) the job moves to PREFLIGHT/TRAINING; the lease dir <repo>/ple_patch/.ainize-teach.lock is released afterwards

**Evidence**

- `packages/node/src/teach.ts trainer-slot lease (mkdir lock, pgrep, nvidia-smi); packages/node/test/teach.test.ts 'slot busy → blocked=slot'`
- `packages/web/src/i18n/pages/teach.ts teach.card.blocked`
